# Plan 9: voice (natural French voice and scored speaking) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking. This plan is executed natively (executing-plans); the final whole-branch review (Task 13) runs **before** the production deploy (Task 14), the order that worked for Plan 8.

**Goal:** A learner records a spoken answer to a speaking task or to the placement test's speaking section and gets a
transcript, four criteria scores, a practice level estimate, corrections and a tip; the AI tutor reads its French
replies aloud in a natural voice and accepts spoken messages; listening items that have only a script get natural
French audio made once and reused; Free and Premium learners use voice within daily limits an administrator can change.

**Architecture:**
- **Relay (Vercel, Node).** One serverless function `parle-app/api/voice.ts` (helpers in `api/_voice/`, which Vercel
  never turns into functions because of the underscore). Actions: `transcribe` (a 16 kHz mono WAV part in the request
  body), `speak` (text of one tutor reply, answered as MP3) and `listen` (a listening item, answered with a link to the
  cached MP3). It talks to NVIDIA's hosted Riva over gRPC (`@grpc/grpc-js`, protos embedded as a generated JSON
  descriptor) and, before every NVIDIA call, asks the backend to take one unit of the learner's limit. The relay holds
  `NVIDIA_API_KEY` and a shared secret `VOICE_RELAY_SECRET`; it never stores audio. Tests use a fake provider.
- **Backend (Supabase).** One migration (000029): `voice_use` (one row per use: the daily counters and the transcript
  of an answer until it is scored), `speaking_result`, `voice_preference`, `listening_audio` (the cache index), a
  private Storage bucket `listening-audio`, and the SQL functions `voice_take()` and `listening_claim()` that must not
  race. A new backend-native function `parle-voice-service` (code in `supabase/functions/_shared/voice/`) answers the
  relay (secret header + the learner's token: `getCaller()` refuses paused accounts and bad tokens) and the app
  (status, read-aloud preference, scoring, saved results, the admin list of listening items without audio).
  Placement speaking is scored inside `parle-plan-service` with the same scoring module.
- **App.** The browser records with `MediaRecorder` (WebM/Opus or Safari's MP4/AAC), decodes with Web Audio, mixes to
  mono, resamples to 16 kHz and sends WAV parts of at most 60 seconds. New modules `voice-text.ts` (shared with the
  server), `voice-audio.ts`, `voice-recorder.ts`, `voice-client.ts`, `voice-playback.ts`; components `VoiceRecorder`,
  `SpeakingResultCard`, `SpeakingTaskPanel`, `ListeningAudioButton`, `AdminListeningAudio`. Speaking tasks on Today,
  the placement speaking screen, the tutor's Listen, "Read replies aloud" and microphone, listening audio buttons, six
  admin settings, the privacy paragraph.

**Tech Stack:** Bun (bun:test backend; app tests in node:test style run by `bun run test`), Supabase (local Docker
stack, Edge Functions on Deno, Postgres 17, Storage), supabase-js 2, Vercel Node functions (Web `Request`/`Response`
handler), `@grpc/grpc-js` 1.14, `@grpc/proto-loader` 0.8, `protobufjs` 7 (build-time only), `@breezystack/lamejs`
(MP3), NVIDIA hosted Riva (Whisper large-v3, Magpie TTS multilingual), React + Vite app, Playwright from the gstack
install (Node, `npx tsx`), Supabase MCP for read-only production checks.

**Spec:** `06_Build/export-decoupling/specs/2026-10-16-plan-9-voice-design.md` (approved by the founder 2026-10-16), all
sections. `specs/SPEC-BACKLOG.md` row 3 (voice). Decisions #16 (split: this plan does audio both ways, not
pronunciation or live voice), #54, #55. Conventions, helpers and quirks: `plans/2026-10-16-plan-8-account-admin-waitlist.md`,
its report, and `plans/2026-10-16-plan-7b-payouts.md` (Global Constraints). Working proof of concept (2026-10-16):
`D:/Temp/claude/D--AgentDevWork-repos-Parle/f7179c44-5247-43a4-8dce-0c6c72f5641d/scratchpad/voice-spike/client/spike.mjs`.

- Production Supabase project `hcphvipeplkfenugikrf`. `waitlist_signup` and `join_waitlist()` are never touched.

  (the token is stripped of its `NAME=` prefix, never printed). Function settings uploads are done by the founder as
  `!` commands. This plan adds one setting: `VOICE_RELAY_SECRET` (a secret, generated by Claude into
  `C:/Users/AgentDev/.parle-secrets/VOICE_RELAY_SECRET`).
- Vercel (app): `bunx vercel deploy --yes --target preview --scope shayans-projects-d00acc44`, then
  `bunx vercel alias set <deployment> parle-preview-shayans-projects-d00acc44.vercel.app --scope shayans-projects-d00acc44`.
  **Never `--prod` for the app.** A first "Not authorized" answer is retried once. The Vercel project settings
  `NVIDIA_API_KEY` and `VOICE_RELAY_SECRET` (Preview and Production) are added by the founder (Task 14 Step 4).
- **NVIDIA:** the key is never sent to the browser, never printed, never committed. Spec values, verbatim: server
  `grpc.nvcf.nvidia.com:443` with metadata `function-id` and `authorization: Bearer <key>`; Synthesize function-id
  `877104f7-e885-42b9-8de8-f6e4c6303969`, voice `Magpie-Multilingual.FR-FR.Louise`, `language_code` `fr-FR`,
  encoding `LINEAR_PCM` (1), `sample_rate_hz` 22050; Recognize function-id `b702f636-f60c-4a3d-a6f4-f3568c13bd7d`,
  `language_code` `"fr"`, WAV input. **No test calls NVIDIA**: locally and in every test the relay runs with
  `VOICE_PROVIDER=fake`, which the relay refuses to use on Vercel. Real NVIDIA calls happen only in Task 15.
- **No audio is stored or logged** except generated listening audio (Parle's own content) in the private bucket
  `listening-audio`. Recordings exist only in the browser and in transit. Transcripts are stored like typed answers and
  go with account deletion.
- Spec values, verbatim: recordings up to 3 minutes (answers) and 1 minute (tutor messages); text up to about 1,000
  characters per `speak` call; provider timeout 30 s, then "Voice is busy. Try again in a moment."; under about 10 words
  or silent: "We couldn't hear enough. Try again."; mostly not French: "Answer in French to get a score."; shape failure
  twice: "We couldn't score this one. Try again."; at the limit: "You've used today's spoken answers. Type your answer,
  or try again tomorrow."; result labels "What we heard", "practice estimate, not an official result",
  "Pronunciation isn't scored yet."; criteria 0 to 4: task completion, vocabulary, grammar, coherence and fluency;
  levels A1 to C1. Limits (Free / Premium): answers 2 / 15, voice messages 5 / 60, replies read aloud 20 / 200;
  placement speaking and cached listening audio never counted. Scoring uses `ai_model_standard` (mode `standard` of
  `_shared/ai.ts`).
- Lessons are in CAD (decision #55); Premium stays USD. **No em dashes in any new user-facing string** (Task 12 adds a
  test). Hyphens in NCLC ranges ("NCLC 3-4").
- Secrets (keys, tokens, passwords, codes, the relay secret, the Vercel bypass) are never printed, logged, committed or
  put literally on a command line (`"$(cat <file>)"` is fine). Test-account passwords live only in

- Files that contain backslashes or regular expressions are written with the editor tools (Write/Edit), never with a
  shell heredoc (Git Bash here collapses backslashes). This applies to most files in this plan.
- `supabase/config.toml` lists a function only once its folder exists (7A ruling): `parle-voice-service` is added in
  Task 3. Restart `bunx supabase functions serve` after any change to `supabase/functions/.env` or
  `supabase/config.toml`, after adding a function folder, and when a new action answers `UNKNOWN_ACTION`.
- After editing `parle-app/functions/*` or an app module the server copies (`src/lib/i18n.ts`, `src/lib/learner-plan.ts`,

  before committing. A sync rewrite can cause a transient 502 or 503 in the next local call: run the test again once.
- Backend tests need the local stack (`bunx supabase start -x imgproxy,vector,logflare,supavisor`, after the founder

  tests get a 30-60 s limit (`setDefaultTimeout(60_000)` at the top of each new backend test file). Under the full
  suite the local edge runtime can log "CPU time soft limit reached": a lone 502 is rerun once before it is treated as
  a failure.
- Backend tests that exercise the relay import its handler in-process from `../../parle-app/api/_voice/handler` with
  `VOICE_PROVIDER=fake` and the local backend; the relay's packages resolve from `parle-app/node_modules`, so
  `cd 07_Engineering/parle-app && bun install` must have run (Task 4).
- Relay files (`parle-app/api/**`) use ESM relative imports **with the `.js` extension** (`./_voice/handler.js`): the
  app's `package.json` is `"type": "module"` and Vercel's Node runtime needs the extension; Bun maps `.js` to the `.ts`
  file in tests. Type-check them with `bunx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.api.json` (0 errors).
- App tests: `cd 07_Engineering/parle-app && bun run test`. New app test files use
  `import assert from "node:assert/strict"; import { describe, it } from "node:test";`. App type errors:
  `bunx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json 2>&1 | grep "error TS" | sed -E 's/\([0-9]+,[0-9]+\)//' | sort > <workspace>/plan9-tsc-<task>.txt`
  and compare with the Task 0 baseline list (`diff`, no new `>` line), not only the count; the count must stay at or

- The local app cannot run on this machine (Windows Smart App Control blocks `vite` and `vite build`). The build is

- R94 (the question bank readers serve demo items only) is **not** fixed here. The listening audio cache is keyed by

## Review Focus

The five failure modes the spec implies that no spec test exercises, most likely first. Each is pinned by a named test
in the task that owns the code.

1. **A silent or noise-only recording.** Whisper is known to invent text on silence ("Sous-titres réalisés par la
   communauté d'Amara.org", "Merci d'avoir regardé"). A learner who pressed Record in a quiet room must see "We couldn't
   hear enough. Try again." and lose nothing: not scored, not counted. Tests: Task 2 "cleanTranscript removes Whisper's
   inventions on silence (Review Focus 1)", Task 8 "a recording without a voice is caught before sending (Review Focus
   1)", Task 5 "a recording where Whisper invented subtitle credits is not scored and not counted (Review Focus 1)".
2. **Send pressed twice, or the browser retries after a slow network.** The answer is scored once, counted once, and
   both presses show the same result. Test: Task 5 "scoring the same recording twice, one after the other or at once,
   gives one result and one count (Review Focus 2)".
3. **A learner practising around their local midnight.** Limits are per day in the learner's own time zone (spec 6):
   a use at 23:59 Toronto time and one at 00:01 count on two different days, whatever UTC says. Test: Task 3 "a use just
   before and just after the learner's local midnight counts on two days (Review Focus 3)".
4. **A long answer split into parts.** A 3-minute answer becomes up to four parts of at most 60 seconds (the Vercel
   body limit); a part that arrives out of order, a fifth part, or a part after the answer was scored or voided must be
   refused, never appended to someone's transcript. Tests: Task 3 "parts arrive in order, at most four, and none after
   the answer is closed (Review Focus 4)", Task 8 "a three-minute recording becomes at most four parts of at most 60
   seconds (Review Focus 4)".
5. **Listen pressed again, or a long reply read in several pieces.** A reply counts once per day however many times it
   is replayed or split; text that is not in the learner's own tutor reply cannot be spoken on their allowance (or the
   relay becomes a free text-to-speech service). Test: Task 3 "a reply read again or in pieces counts once; text that is
   not in the learner's own reply is refused (Review Focus 5)".

## Design choices and rulings made while writing this plan (for the founder to confirm)

Each item says what it costs if it is wrong.

1. **The browser, not the relay, converts recordings to 16 kHz mono WAV** (spec 3 says the relay converts). The browser
   records WebM/Opus (Chrome, Firefox, Edge) or MP4/AAC (Safari, iPhone), decodes it with Web Audio (every browser
   decodes its own recordings), mixes to mono and resamples to 16 kHz. The alternative, `ffmpeg-static` in the
   function, adds a binary of about 75 MB to every cold start of the relay, needs a Linux binary bundled through
   `includeFiles` and a Windows one for local tests. Cost if wrong: a browser that cannot decode its own recording
   offers typing instead; ffmpeg can be added later behind the same `transcribe` action without changing the app's
   calls.
2. **Recordings are sent in parts of at most 60 seconds** (up to four parts for a 3-minute answer, cut at the quietest
   moment of the last 10 seconds of each part). A 3-minute 16 kHz WAV is 5.8 MB, above Vercel's 4.5 MB request limit
   and gRPC's usual 4 MB message limit; 60 seconds is 1.9 MB. The transcripts are joined on the server. Cost if wrong: a
   word at a cut can be misheard (the cut is placed in a pause).
3. **The relay checks the learner through the backend.** Its first backend call (`voice_take` or `listening_claim`)
   carries the learner's token; the backend's `getCaller()` refuses a bad token, an unconfirmed email and a paused
   account (Plan 8), and the relay calls NVIDIA only after that answer. This is the spec's `auth.getUser` check, done
   once, in the code that already handles pauses. Cost if wrong: none for security; one design to explain.
4. **A new shared secret `VOICE_RELAY_SECRET`** (Vercel setting and Supabase function setting) lets only the relay take
   units and save transcripts. Two places now hold it as well as the NVIDIA key (spec risk 5): rotate together. Cost if
   wrong: a leaked secret lets someone spend learners' allowances only with their own tokens.
5. **Replies are sent to the browser as MP3 at 48 kbit/s** (encoded in the relay with `@breezystack/lamejs`, pure
   JavaScript, about 6 KB/s of audio), not WAV (44 KB/s). "Listen slowly" plays the same audio at 0.75 speed. The relay
   speaks at most 400 characters per NVIDIA call (Claude could not confirm Magpie's per-request limit) and joins the
   audio. Cost if wrong: slower first audio on long replies.
6. **Counting.** An answer takes its unit when its first part is transcribed and stays "pending" until it is scored;
   too short, not French or a provider failure gives the unit back; an AI scoring failure keeps the unit but "Try again"
   rescored the same transcript at no new cost. A voice message is counted when transcribed (given back if nothing was
   heard or the provider failed), whether or not the learner then sends it. A reply counts once per reply per day,
   however often it is replayed (replays are capped at 100 an hour per learner). Placement speaking is never counted
   but limited to 6 recordings per placement run. Cached listening audio is never counted; making new audio is capped
   at 60 items an hour per learner (2,000 for administrators). Cost if wrong: a learner who discards a voice message
   loses one of five.
7. **Six settings, named like the AI ones:** `free_voice_answers_per_day` 2, `premium_voice_answers_per_day` 15,
   `free_voice_messages_per_day` 5, `premium_voice_messages_per_day` 60, `free_voice_replies_per_day` 20,
   `premium_voice_replies_per_day` 200 (the spec names `voice_answers_per_day` etc. with a Free and a Premium value).
   Premium may never be set below Free. Cost if wrong: a rename.
8. **Premium is read the way the admin user detail reads it (Plan 8):** a Stripe subscription `active`, `trialing` or
   `past_due`, or the complimentary flag. Cost if wrong: a learner in a Stripe grace period keeps Premium voice limits.
9. **The learner's day** uses the same time zone order as the AI tutor: their Parle profile, then their learner
   profile, then UTC. Cost if wrong: none (same as the AI message counter).
10. **A scored speaking task moves the speaking estimate one step of at most 0.25 toward the scored level** (A1 = 1 ...
    C1 = 5 on the planning scale), marks speaking "assessed" and marks the task done, like the check-in's capped
    steps. A typed answer (microphone refused) is scored with the same rubric, marked "typed, scored as writing
    practice", marks the task done and **does not** change the speaking level. Cost if wrong: one rule.
11. **Placement speaking uses one prompt** (the first speaking prompt at the estimated level, as writing does). C1 is
    stored as B2 in the placement levels (the bank's top band, like the other skills) while the screen shows C1.
    "Finish without speaking" keeps speaking "not yet assessed"; typing is not offered in placement (a typed answer is
    not a speaking placement). Cost if wrong: one screen.
12. **NCLC on every speaking result, for every track**, from the placement mapping: A1 NCLC 2, A2 NCLC 3-4, B1 NCLC
    5-7, B2 NCLC 8-9, C1 NCLC 10 or above. Cost if wrong: display only.
13. **The tutor's microphone stays where it already was, in speaking practice**, replacing the browser's speech
    recognition with the relay (Whisper); the other modes stay typed. The transcript lands in the message box with the
    existing "Confirm transcript" step. Cost if wrong: one button added later to chat mode.
14. **"Read replies aloud" is saved on the server** (`voice_preference`), so it follows the learner across devices.
    Cost if wrong: none.
15. **Listening audio is made for approved `question_item` listening items and for the learner's own
    `listening_exercise` tasks**, stored in a private bucket and served by links valid for one hour. The placement
    listening section gets a player above the transcript; its scoring and its "transcript only" label do not change in
    Plan 9 (a placement scoring decision). Cost if wrong: listening still reads as "transcript only".
16. **Speaking results live in a new table `speaking_result`** (no new columns on Buildy-era tables). Cost if wrong:
    none.
17. **"Mostly not French"** is decided twice: a word-list check on the transcript before any AI cost, then the scoring
    model's `is_french` flag. Cost if wrong: a borderline answer gets "Answer in French to get a score."
18. **`vercel.json`**: the page rewrite now excludes `/api/`, the relay may run 60 s, and the content security policy
    gains `media-src 'self' blob: https://hcphvipeplkfenugikrf.supabase.co` (today audio from `blob:` and Storage is
    blocked). Cost if wrong: none.
19. **Privacy wording:** the app's privacy page is still a "Coming soon" shell, so the voice paragraph is shown on it
    under the shell text, and the AI tutor information page gains one line. parle.buzz's privacy page covers only the
    waitlist and is unchanged. The lawyer review (D28) includes the paragraph. Cost if wrong: one more page to edit.
20. **Speaking task cards keep "Mark done" and the tutor link**; the recorder is added, not substituted. The weekly
    check-in's typed speaking reflection is unchanged (not in the spec). Cost if wrong: none.

### Task 1: Data layer: voice counters, speaking results, the read-aloud preference, the listening audio cache

**Files:**
- Create: `parle-backend/supabase/migrations/20261016000029_voice.sql`, `parle-backend/tests/voice-schema.test.ts`
- Modify: `parle-app/entities/skill_estimate.json` (the `source` enum gains `speaking_task`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `auth.users`, `public.skill_estimate` (its `source` check), `storage.buckets`.
- Produces tables (row-level security on, no browser grant):
  - `voice_use (id uuid pk, user_id uuid -> auth.users on delete cascade, kind 'answer'|'message'|'reply'|'placement',
    day date, ref text, status 'pending'|'counted'|'void', void_reason ''|'too_short'|'not_french'|'provider_failed'|'empty',
    parts int 0..4, transcript text, duration_ms int 0..200000, created_at, settled_at)`; a reply is unique per
    `(user_id, day, ref)`;
  - `speaking_result (id, user_id -> auth.users cascade, source 'task'|'placement', mode 'spoken'|'typed', task_id text,
    placement_run_id uuid, ticket_id uuid unique -> voice_use on delete set null, prompt_text, transcript, criteria jsonb,
    level 'A1'..'C1', corrections jsonb, tip, duration_ms, word_count, words_per_minute int null, created_at)`;
  - `voice_preference (user_id pk -> auth.users cascade, read_aloud bool, updated_at)`;
  - `listening_audio (cache_key text pk, item_kind 'question_item'|'learner_task', item_id text, owner_id uuid null ->
    auth.users cascade, script_hash text, path text, status 'pending'|'ready', claimed_at, ready_at)`.
- Produces SQL functions (service role only):
  - `voice_take(p_user uuid, p_kind text, p_day date, p_limit int, p_ref text) returns jsonb`:
    `{ ok: true, ticket_id, used, again }` or `{ ok: false, used }`; answers and placement start `pending`, messages and
    replies `counted`; a reply already taken that day returns its ticket with `again: true` and takes nothing; placement
    counts per `ref` (the run) over all days; others count per day, without `void` rows.
  - `listening_claim(p_key text, p_kind text, p_item text, p_owner uuid, p_hash text) returns jsonb`:
    `{ state: 'claimed' }` (make it now), `{ state: 'busy' }` (someone is making it), `{ state: 'ready', path }`.
    A pending claim older than 90 seconds can be claimed again.
- Produces the private bucket `listening-audio` (MP3 only, 10 MB a file).
- `skill_estimate.source` accepts `speaking_task`.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, setDefaultTimeout } from "bun:test";
import { createClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { LOCAL_URL, localKeys } from "./helpers/local";
import { makeUser } from "./helpers/users";

setDefaultTimeout(60_000);
const service = () => createClient(LOCAL_URL, localKeys().service, { auth: { persistSession: false } });
const psql = (sql: string) => Bun.spawnSync(["docker", "exec", "supabase_db_parle-staging", "psql", "-U", "postgres", "-tA", "-c", sql]).stdout.toString().trim();
type Take = { ok: boolean; ticket_id?: string; used: number; again?: boolean };
async function take(user: string, kind: string, day: string, limit: number, ref = ""): Promise<Take> {
  const { data, error } = await service().rpc("voice_take", { p_user: user, p_kind: kind, p_day: day, p_limit: limit, p_ref: ref });
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
  return data as Take;
}
const DAY = "2026-10-16";

describe("voice schema (Plan 9)", () => {
  it("the four tables are server-only: row-level security on, a learner reads nothing", async () => {
    for (const t of ["voice_use", "speaking_result", "voice_preference", "listening_audio"]) {
      expect(`${t}: ${psql(`select relrowsecurity from pg_class where relname = '${t}' and relnamespace = 'public'::regnamespace`)}`).toBe(`${t}: t`);
    }
    const u = await makeUser("learner");
    await take(u.id, "message", DAY, 5);
    const { data } = await u.client.from("voice_use").select("id");
    expect(data ?? []).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("counts up to the limit, per kind and per day; a given-back use does not count", async () => {
    const u = await makeUser("learner");
    expect((await take(u.id, "answer", DAY, 2)).ok).toBe(true);
    const second = await take(u.id, "answer", DAY, 2);
    expect(second).toMatchObject({ ok: true, used: 2 });
    expect(await take(u.id, "answer", DAY, 2)).toEqual({ ok: false, used: 2 });
    expect((await take(u.id, "answer", "2026-10-17", 2)).ok).toBe(true);
    expect((await take(u.id, "message", DAY, 5)).ok).toBe(true);
    await service().from("voice_use").update({ status: "void", void_reason: "too_short" }).eq("id", second.ticket_id!);
    expect((await take(u.id, "answer", DAY, 2)).ok).toBe(true);
  });
  it("answers and placement recordings start pending; messages and replies are counted at once", async () => {
    const u = await makeUser("learner");
    const ids = [await take(u.id, "answer", DAY, 2), await take(u.id, "placement", DAY, 6, "run-a"), await take(u.id, "message", DAY, 5), await take(u.id, "reply", DAY, 20, "m-1")].map((r) => r.ticket_id!);
    const { data } = await service().from("voice_use").select("id, status").in("id", ids);
    expect(ids.map((id) => data!.find((r) => r.id === id)!.status)).toEqual(["pending", "pending", "counted", "counted"]);
  });
  it("a reply is counted once per day however often it is taken", async () => {
    const u = await makeUser("learner");
    const a = await take(u.id, "reply", DAY, 20, "m-1");
    const b = await take(u.id, "reply", DAY, 20, "m-1");
    expect(b).toMatchObject({ ok: true, again: true, ticket_id: a.ticket_id, used: 1 });
    expect((await take(u.id, "reply", "2026-10-17", 20, "m-1")).again).toBe(false);
  });
  it("placement recordings are limited per placement run, not per day", async () => {
    const u = await makeUser("learner");
    expect((await take(u.id, "placement", DAY, 2, "run-1")).ok).toBe(true);
    expect((await take(u.id, "placement", "2026-10-17", 2, "run-1")).ok).toBe(true);
    expect((await take(u.id, "placement", "2026-10-18", 2, "run-1")).ok).toBe(false);
    expect((await take(u.id, "placement", DAY, 2, "run-2")).ok).toBe(true);
  });
  it("five uses at once never pass a limit of two", async () => {
    const u = await makeUser("learner");
    const results = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 5 }, () => take(u.id, "message", DAY, 2)));
    expect(results.filter((r) => r.ok).length).toBe(2);
  });
  it("listening audio is claimed once: the first request makes it, the next waits, then everyone gets the file", async () => {
    const key = `question_item:${crypto.randomUUID()}:abc`;
    const claim = async () => (await service().rpc("listening_claim", { p_key: key, p_kind: "question_item", p_item: "x", p_owner: null, p_hash: "abc" })).data;
    expect(await claim()).toEqual({ state: "claimed" });
    expect(await claim()).toEqual({ state: "busy" });
    await service().from("listening_audio").update({ status: "ready", path: "bank/x/abc.mp3" }).eq("cache_key", key);
    expect(await claim()).toEqual({ state: "ready", path: "bank/x/abc.mp3" });
    psql(`update public.listening_audio set status = 'pending', claimed_at = now() - interval '2 minutes' where cache_key = '${key}'`);
    expect(await claim()).toEqual({ state: "claimed" });
  });
  it("a deleted login takes its voice rows, speaking results and preference with it", async () => {
    const u = await makeUser("learner");
    const t = await take(u.id, "answer", DAY, 2);
    const r = await service().from("speaking_result").insert({ user_id: u.id, source: "task", task_id: "t", ticket_id: t.ticket_id, transcript: "Bonjour", criteria: { task_completion: 1, vocabulary: 1, grammar: 1, coherence_fluency: 1 }, level: "A1" });
    expect(r.error).toBeNull();
    expect((await service().from("voice_preference").insert({ user_id: u.id, read_aloud: true })).error).toBeNull();
    await service().auth.admin.deleteUser(u.id);
    for (const table of ["voice_use", "speaking_result", "voice_preference"]) {
      expect(`${table}: ${psql(`select count(*) from public.${table} where user_id = '${u.id}'`)}`).toBe(`${table}: 0`);
    }
  });
  it("the listening-audio bucket is private and takes only MP3; speaking estimates accept their new source", () => {
    expect(psql("select public, allowed_mime_types from storage.buckets where id = 'listening-audio'")).toBe("f|{audio/mpeg}");
    expect(psql("select pg_get_constraintdef(oid) from pg_constraint where conname = 'skill_estimate_source_check'")).toContain("speaking_task");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the migration.** Create `parle-backend/supabase/migrations/20261016000029_voice.sql` (editor tool):

```sql
-- Plan 9 (spec 06_Build/export-decoupling/specs/2026-10-16-plan-9-voice-design.md): voice counters, speaking results,
-- the read-aloud preference and the listening audio cache. Recordings are never stored: only transcripts (like typed
-- answers) and generated listening audio (Parle's own content). Every table is server-only. Settings keep their
-- defaults in code (parle-admin-settings.helper.ts): this migration writes no app_setting row.

-- One row per use of the voice relay. Answers and placement recordings stay 'pending' (holding the transcript) until
-- they are scored; 'void' rows (too short, not French, provider failure, nothing heard) do not count.
create table public.voice_use (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  kind text not null check (kind in ('answer', 'message', 'reply', 'placement')),
  day date not null,
  ref text not null default '' check (char_length(ref) <= 200),
  status text not null default 'counted' check (status in ('pending', 'counted', 'void')),
  void_reason text not null default '' check (void_reason in ('', 'too_short', 'not_french', 'provider_failed', 'empty')),
  parts integer not null default 0 check (parts between 0 and 4),
  transcript text not null default '' check (char_length(transcript) <= 6000),
  duration_ms integer not null default 0 check (duration_ms between 0 and 200000),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  settled_at timestamptz
);
comment on table public.voice_use is 'Plan 9: voice uses per learner and day (the daily limits); an answer keeps its transcript until scored. Server-only.';
create index voice_use_count on public.voice_use (user_id, kind, day);
create unique index voice_use_reply_once on public.voice_use (user_id, day, ref) where kind = 'reply';
alter table public.voice_use enable row level security;
revoke all on public.voice_use from anon, authenticated;

create table public.speaking_result (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  source text not null check (source in ('task', 'placement')),
  mode text not null default 'spoken' check (mode in ('spoken', 'typed')),
  task_id text not null default '',
  placement_run_id uuid,
  ticket_id uuid unique references public.voice_use(id) on delete set null,
  prompt_text text not null default '' check (char_length(prompt_text) <= 2000),
  transcript text not null check (char_length(transcript) <= 6000),
  criteria jsonb not null,
  level text not null check (level in ('A1', 'A2', 'B1', 'B2', 'C1')),
  corrections jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb,
  tip text not null default '' check (char_length(tip) <= 600),
  duration_ms integer not null default 0,
  word_count integer not null default 0,
  words_per_minute integer,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
comment on table public.speaking_result is 'Plan 9: scored speaking answers (transcript, four criteria, level, corrections, tip). No audio. Server-only.';
create index speaking_result_task on public.speaking_result (user_id, task_id);
alter table public.speaking_result enable row level security;
revoke all on public.speaking_result from anon, authenticated;

create table public.voice_preference (
  user_id uuid primary key references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  read_aloud boolean not null default false,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
comment on table public.voice_preference is 'Plan 9: the tutor''s "Read replies aloud" switch, per learner. Server-only.';
alter table public.voice_preference enable row level security;
revoke all on public.voice_preference from anon, authenticated;

-- Generated listening audio, keyed by the item and a hash of its script (works for real bank items, R94).
create table public.listening_audio (
  cache_key text primary key check (char_length(cache_key) <= 200),
  item_kind text not null check (item_kind in ('question_item', 'learner_task')),
  item_id text not null,
  owner_id uuid references auth.users(id) on delete cascade, -- set for a learner's own task audio
  script_hash text not null,
  path text not null default '',
  status text not null default 'pending' check (status in ('pending', 'ready')),
  claimed_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  ready_at timestamptz
);
comment on table public.listening_audio is 'Plan 9: index of generated listening audio in the private bucket listening-audio. Server-only.';
alter table public.listening_audio enable row level security;
revoke all on public.listening_audio from anon, authenticated;

insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public, file_size_limit, allowed_mime_types)
values ('listening-audio', 'listening-audio', false, 10485760, array['audio/mpeg'])
on conflict (id) do nothing;

-- A scored speaking answer moves the speaking estimate (Plan 9 ruling 10). Same check as 20261014000001, one value added.
alter table public."skill_estimate" drop constraint if exists "skill_estimate_source_check";
alter table public."skill_estimate" add constraint "skill_estimate_source_check"
  check ("source" in ('placement','self_assessment','self_assessment_fallback','checkin','retake','speaking_task',''));

-- Takes one use of a kind for a learner's day, under the limit, one learner and kind at a time.
create or replace function public.voice_take(p_user uuid, p_kind text, p_day date, p_limit int, p_ref text)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v_used int; v_id uuid;
begin
  if p_user is null or p_day is null or p_kind not in ('answer', 'message', 'reply', 'placement') or p_limit is null or p_limit < 0 then
    raise exception 'voice_take: user, day, a known kind and a limit of zero or more are required';
  end if;
  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('voice:' || p_user::text || ':' || p_kind, 0));
  if p_kind = 'reply' then
    select id into v_id from public.voice_use where user_id = p_user and kind = 'reply' and day = p_day and ref = coalesce(p_ref, '');
    if v_id is not null then
      select count(*) into v_used from public.voice_use where user_id = p_user and kind = 'reply' and day = p_day and status <> 'void';
      return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'ticket_id', v_id, 'used', v_used, 'again', true);
    end if;
  end if;
  if p_kind = 'placement' then
    select count(*) into v_used from public.voice_use where user_id = p_user and kind = 'placement' and ref = coalesce(p_ref, '');
  else
    select count(*) into v_used from public.voice_use where user_id = p_user and kind = p_kind and day = p_day and status <> 'void';
  end if;
  if v_used >= p_limit then
    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'used', v_used);
  end if;
  insert into public.voice_use (user_id, kind, day, ref, status)
  values (p_user, p_kind, p_day, coalesce(p_ref, ''), case when p_kind in ('answer', 'placement') then 'pending' else 'counted' end)
  returning id into v_id;
  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'ticket_id', v_id, 'used', v_used + 1, 'again', false);
end; $$;
revoke all on function public.voice_take(uuid, text, date, int, text) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.voice_take(uuid, text, date, int, text) to service_role;

-- One generation per listening item and script, even when two learners open it at once.
create or replace function public.listening_claim(p_key text, p_kind text, p_item text, p_owner uuid, p_hash text)
returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v public.listening_audio;
begin
  insert into public.listening_audio (cache_key, item_kind, item_id, owner_id, script_hash)
  values (p_key, p_kind, p_item, p_owner, p_hash)
  on conflict (cache_key) do nothing;
  if found then
    return jsonb_build_object('state', 'claimed');
  end if;
  select * into v from public.listening_audio where cache_key = p_key for update;
  if v.status = 'ready' then
    return jsonb_build_object('state', 'ready', 'path', v.path);
  end if;
  if v.claimed_at < now() - interval '90 seconds' then
    update public.listening_audio set claimed_at = now() where cache_key = p_key;
    return jsonb_build_object('state', 'claimed');
  end if;
  return jsonb_build_object('state', 'busy');
end; $$;
revoke all on function public.listening_claim(text, text, text, uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.listening_claim(text, text, text, uuid, text) to service_role;
```

  In `parle-app/entities/skill_estimate.json`, replace
  `"enum": ["placement", "self_assessment", "self_assessment_fallback", "checkin", "retake"]` with
  `"enum": ["placement", "self_assessment", "self_assessment_fallback", "checkin", "retake", "speaking_task"]`.

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { describe, it } from "node:test";
import {
  cleanTranscript,
  countSpokenWords,
  frenchChunks,
  looksFrench,
  mostlyNotFrench,
  nextSpeakingEstimate,
  placementSpeakingLevel,
  speakingNclc,
  spokenTextIsFrom,
  wordsPerMinute,
} from "./voice-text";

describe("voice text rules (Plan 9)", () => {
  it("cleanTranscript removes Whisper's inventions on silence (Review Focus 1)", () => {
    assert.equal(cleanTranscript("Sous-titres réalisés par la communauté d'Amara.org"), "");
    assert.equal(cleanTranscript("Sous-titrage ST' 501"), "");
    assert.equal(cleanTranscript("Merci d'avoir regardé cette vidéo !"), "");
    assert.equal(cleanTranscript("[Musique] ♪♪"), "");
    assert.equal(cleanTranscript("Je travaille à Montréal. Sous-titres réalisés par la communauté d'Amara.org"), "Je travaille à Montréal.");
    assert.equal(cleanTranscript("  Je   suis\nici. "), "Je suis ici.");
  });
  it("counts spoken words", () => {
    assert.equal(countSpokenWords(""), 0);
    assert.equal(countSpokenWords(" Je suis allé au marché. "), 5);
  });
  it("tells French from English", () => {
    assert.equal(looksFrench("Je suis allé au marché avec ma sœur."), true);
    assert.equal(looksFrench("Great job! Here is a small correction."), false);
    assert.equal(looksFrench("Bonjour !"), true);
    assert.equal(mostlyNotFrench("Hello, I went to the market with my sister and we bought bread."), true);
    assert.equal(mostlyNotFrench("Le week-end dernier, je suis allé au marché avec ma sœur."), false);
    assert.equal(mostlyNotFrench("Je suis allé au market, it was nice."), false);
  });
  it("frenchChunks keeps only the French sentences, in order, without markdown, in chunks of at most 1,000 characters", () => {
    const reply = "Great job!\n**Correction :** Je suis allé au marché.\n- Nous avons acheté du pain.\nRemember: allé takes être.";
    assert.deepEqual(frenchChunks(reply), ["Correction : Je suis allé au marché. Nous avons acheté du pain."]);
    const long = Array.from({ length: 60 }, (_, i) => `Je suis allé au marché numéro ${i} avec ma sœur.`).join(" ");
    const chunks = frenchChunks(long);
    assert.ok(chunks.length > 1);
    assert.ok(chunks.every((c) => c.length <= 1000));
    assert.equal(chunks.join(" "), long);
  });
  it("every chunk of a reply is recognised as coming from that reply, and other text is not", () => {
    const reply = "Très bien ! Here is a small correction.\nJe suis allé au marché. Nous avons acheté du pain";
    for (const chunk of frenchChunks(reply)) assert.equal(spokenTextIsFrom(chunk, reply), true);
    assert.equal(spokenTextIsFrom("Bonjour, je vends des voitures pas chères.", reply), false);
    assert.equal(spokenTextIsFrom("", reply), false);
  });
  it("maps levels to NCLC, the placement band, the speaking rate and the next estimate", () => {
    assert.deepEqual(["A1", "A2", "B1", "B2", "C1"].map((l) => speakingNclc(l as never)), ["NCLC 2", "NCLC 3-4", "NCLC 5-7", "NCLC 8-9", "NCLC 10 or above"]);
    assert.equal(placementSpeakingLevel("C1"), "B2");
    assert.equal(placementSpeakingLevel("A2"), "A2");
    assert.equal(wordsPerMinute(60, 30_000), 120);
    assert.equal(wordsPerMinute(3, 2_000), null);
    assert.equal(nextSpeakingEstimate(2, "B1"), 2.25);
    assert.equal(nextSpeakingEstimate(3, "A1"), 2.75);
    assert.equal(nextSpeakingEstimate(4, "B2"), 4);
    assert.equal(nextSpeakingEstimate(2.1, "B2"), 2.25);
  });
});
```

```ts
/**
 * Plan 9: text rules shared by the app and the server (copied to supabase/functions/_app by
 * parle-backend/scripts/sync-app-code.ts). Pure functions only, no imports.
 */
export type SpeakingLevel = "A1" | "A2" | "B1" | "B2" | "C1";
export const SPEAKING_LEVELS: readonly SpeakingLevel[] = ["A1", "A2", "B1", "B2", "C1"];
/** Spec 4.3: "Under about 10 words or silent: not scored, not counted". */
export const MIN_ANSWER_WORDS = 10;
/** Spec 3: text up to about 1,000 characters per call; longer replies are split by the app. */
export const MAX_SPEAK_CHARS = 1000;

export function countSpokenWords(text: string): number {
  const trimmed = String(text ?? "").trim();
  return trimmed ? trimmed.split(/\s+/u).filter(Boolean).length : 0;
}

/** Whisper's well-known inventions on silence or noise (subtitle credits, video sign-offs, sound tags). */
const INVENTIONS: RegExp[] = [
  /sous-titr\p{L}*[^!?\n]*?(?:amara\.org|st'?\s*\d+|radio-canada|communaut[ée](?:\s+d'amara\.org)?)[.!?]?/giu,
  /merci d'avoir regard[ée](?: cette vid[ée]o)?\s*[.!]?/giu,
  /abonnez-vous[^.!?]*[.!?]?/giu,
  /\[(?:musique|music|applaudissements|rires|silence|bruit)\]/giu,
  /♪+/gu,
];

export function cleanTranscript(raw: string): string {
  let text = String(raw ?? "").replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/g, " ");
  for (const pattern of INVENTIONS) text = text.replace(pattern, " ");
  return text.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
}

const FRENCH_WORDS = new Set([
  "le", "la", "les", "un", "une", "des", "du", "de", "et", "est", "je", "j", "tu", "il", "elle", "nous", "vous", "ils",
  "elles", "que", "qu", "qui", "pas", "ne", "n", "ce", "c", "cette", "mon", "ma", "mes", "ton", "ta", "son", "sa",
  "leur", "au", "aux", "avec", "pour", "dans", "sur", "mais", "ou", "où", "donc", "car", "très", "suis", "sont", "ai",
  "avons", "avez", "ont", "été", "être", "fait", "y", "parce", "quand", "comme", "bien", "aussi", "moi", "chez",
  "bonjour", "merci", "oui", "non", "voici", "beaucoup", "maintenant", "aujourd", "hui", "travail", "j'ai",
]);
const ENGLISH_WORDS = new Set([
  "the", "an", "and", "is", "are", "was", "were", "i", "you", "he", "she", "we", "they", "it", "that", "this", "to",
  "of", "in", "with", "for", "not", "have", "has", "be", "my", "your", "but", "or", "so", "because", "what", "does",
  "did", "will", "would", "can", "there", "here", "yes", "hello", "thanks", "which", "from", "at", "by", "about",
  "just", "like", "great", "job", "remember", "takes", "went", "bought", "sister", "market",
]);

function wordsOf(text: string): string[] {
  return String(text ?? "").normalize("NFC").toLocaleLowerCase("fr").split(/[^\p{L}]+/u).filter(Boolean);
}

export function languageCounts(text: string): { french: number; english: number } {
  let french = 0, english = 0;
  for (const word of wordsOf(text)) {
    if (FRENCH_WORDS.has(word)) french++;
    else if (ENGLISH_WORDS.has(word)) english++;
  }
  return { french, english };
}

/** A sentence the French voice should read (Magpie speaks France French only, spec 5). */
export function looksFrench(text: string): boolean {
  const { french, english } = languageCounts(text);
  if (french + english === 0) return /[àâçéèêëîïôûùüÿœæ]/iu.test(text);
  return french >= english;
}

/** A transcript that is mostly not French (spec 4.3): checked before any AI cost. */
export function mostlyNotFrench(text: string): boolean {
  const { french, english } = languageCounts(text);
  return english >= 3 && english > french * 1.5;
}

/** Lines of a reply as they are spoken: list markers and markdown symbols removed, empty lines dropped. */
export function speechLines(text: string): string[] {
  return String(text ?? "")
    .split(/\r?\n+/)
    .map((line) => line.replace(/^\s*(?:[-•*]|\d+[.)])\s+/u, "").replace(/[*_#`>|~]+/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
}

export function normalizeForSpeech(text: string): string {
  return speechLines(text).join(" ");
}

export function splitSentences(line: string): string[] {
  return String(line ?? "").split(/(?<=[.!?…])\s+/u).map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
}

function hardSplit(sentence: string, max: number): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  let rest = sentence;
  while (rest.length > max) {
    const space = rest.lastIndexOf(" ", max);
    const at = space > max / 2 ? space : max;
    out.push(rest.slice(0, at).trim());
    rest = rest.slice(at).trim();
  }
  if (rest) out.push(rest);
  return out;
}

/**
 * The French sentences of a reply, in order, grouped into chunks of at most `max` characters. A sentence without
 * closing punctuation always ends its chunk, so every sentence of a chunk is found as written in the reply (the
 * server checks this with spokenTextIsFrom).
 */
export function frenchChunks(text: string, max = MAX_SPEAK_CHARS): string[] {
  const pieces = speechLines(text).flatMap(splitSentences).filter(looksFrench).flatMap((s) => (s.length <= max ? [s] : hardSplit(s, max)));
  const chunks: string[] = [];
  let current = "";
  for (const piece of pieces) {
    const closed = /[.!?…]$/u.test(current);
    if (current && (!closed || current.length + 1 + piece.length > max)) {
      chunks.push(current);
      current = piece;
    } else {
      current = current ? `${current} ${piece}` : piece;
    }
  }
  if (current) chunks.push(current);
  return chunks;
}

/** True when every sentence of `text` appears, as written, in `source` (a tutor reply). */
export function spokenTextIsFrom(text: string, source: string): boolean {
  const whole = normalizeForSpeech(source).toLocaleLowerCase("fr");
  const sentences = speechLines(text).flatMap(splitSentences);
  return sentences.length > 0 && sentences.every((s) => whole.includes(s.toLocaleLowerCase("fr")));
}

const NCLC: Record<SpeakingLevel, string> = { A1: "NCLC 2", A2: "NCLC 3-4", B1: "NCLC 5-7", B2: "NCLC 8-9", C1: "NCLC 10 or above" };
/** The NCLC equivalent shown with every speaking estimate (Plan 9 ruling 12; same bands as the placement result). */
export function speakingNclc(level: SpeakingLevel): string {
  return NCLC[level];
}

/** The placement bank's bands stop at B2 (Plan 9 ruling 11). */
export function placementSpeakingLevel(level: SpeakingLevel): "A1" | "A2" | "B1" | "B2" {
  return level === "C1" ? "B2" : level;
}

/** Words per minute, measured from the recording's length (spec 4.2); null for clips under 5 seconds. */
export function wordsPerMinute(words: number, durationMs: number): number | null {
  return durationMs >= 5000 ? Math.round(words / (durationMs / 60000)) : null;
}

const LEVEL_SCORE: Record<SpeakingLevel, number> = { A1: 1, A2: 2, B1: 3, B2: 4, C1: 5 };
/** One step of at most 0.25 toward the scored level, on the 0-5 planning scale in quarter steps (ruling 10). */
export function nextSpeakingEstimate(current: number, level: SpeakingLevel): number {
  const from = Number.isFinite(current) ? Math.min(5, Math.max(0, current)) : 0;
  const step = Math.max(-0.25, Math.min(0.25, LEVEL_SCORE[level] - from));
  return Math.min(5, Math.max(0, Math.round((from + step) * 4) / 4));
}
```

```ts
import { createClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { LOCAL_URL, localKeys } from "./local";
import { callFunction, functionsEnv } from "./functions-env";
import { onboardedLearner } from "./learners";

export const service = () => createClient(LOCAL_URL, localKeys().service, { auth: { persistSession: false } });
export const FRENCH_ANSWER = "Le week-end dernier, je suis allé au marché avec ma sœur. Nous avons acheté des légumes frais et du pain, puis nous avons pris un café sur la terrasse parce qu'il faisait très beau.";
export const relayHeaders = () => ({ "x-voice-secret": functionsEnv().VOICE_RELAY_SECRET });

/** parle-voice-service the way the app calls it, or (relay = true) the way the relay calls it. */
export const voice = (token: string, body: Record<string, unknown>, relay = false) => callFunction("parle-voice-service", token, body, relay ? relayHeaders() : {});

/** An onboarded learner (America/Toronto) with an open speaking task: the plan's own, or a task turned into one. */
export async function learnerWithSpeakingTask() {
  const { u, t } = await onboardedLearner();
  const plan = (await callFunction("parle-plan-service", t, { action: "get_or_create_plan", options: {} })).body.result;
  const open = (x: any) => x.status === "planned" || x.status === "rolled";
  let task = plan.tasks.find((x: any) => (x.type === "speaking_check" || x.type === "speaking_practice") && open(x));
  if (!task) {
    task = plan.tasks.find((x: any) => x.type !== "weekly_checkin" && x.type !== "tutor_lesson" && open(x));
    const { error } = await service().from("learner_task").update({ type: "speaking_practice", skill: "speaking", status: "planned" }).eq("id", task.id);
    if (error) throw error;
  }
  const { data, error } = await service().from("learner_task").select("id, roadmap_id").eq("id", task.id).single();
  if (error) throw error;
  return { u, t, taskId: String(data.id), roadmapId: String(data.roadmap_id) };
}

/** Takes an answer unit and saves one transcript part, as the relay does after Whisper answers. */
export async function recordedAnswer(t: string, ref: string, transcript = FRENCH_ANSWER, durationMs = 30_000, kind = "answer") {
  const take = await voice(t, { action: "voice_take", kind, ref }, true);
  if (take.status !== 200) return { take, part: null, ticketId: "" };
  const part = await voice(t, { action: "voice_part", ticket_id: take.body.ticket_id, part: 1, transcript, duration_ms: durationMs }, true);
  return { take, part, ticketId: String(take.body.ticket_id) };
}

export async function tutorSession(userId: string, email: string): Promise<string> {
  const { data, error } = await service().from("ai_session").insert({ owner_id: userId, created_by: email, user_id: userId, mode: "speaking", skill: "speaking", topic: "" }).select("id").single();
  if (error) throw error;
  return String(data.id);
}

export async function tutorReply(userId: string, email: string, text: string): Promise<string> {
  const session = await tutorSession(userId, email);
  const { data, error } = await service().from("ai_message").insert({ owner_id: userId, created_by: email, user_id: userId, session_id: session, sender: "tutor_ai", text, transcript: "", feedback_json: "{}", skill: "speaking", topic: "" }).select("id").single();
  if (error) throw error;
  return String(data.id);
}

export async function makePremium(userId: string) {
  const { error } = await service().from("ParleIdentity").update({ premium_comp_active: true }).eq("owner_id", userId);
  if (error) throw error;
}

/** Saves a setting as the admin page does (newest row wins); returns the undo. */
export async function setSetting(key: string, value: number): Promise<() => Promise<void>> {
  const { data, error } = await service().from("app_setting").insert({ key, value: JSON.stringify(value), updated_by: "plan9-test" }).select("id").single();
  if (error) throw error;
  return async () => { await service().from("app_setting").delete().eq("id", data.id); };
}

export async function pause(userId: string) {
  const { error } = await service().auth.admin.updateUserById(userId, { ban_duration: "876000h" });
  if (error) throw error;
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, setDefaultTimeout } from "bun:test";
import { seedLocalContent } from "../scripts/seed-local-content";
import { onboardedLearner } from "./helpers/learners";
import { learnerDay } from "../supabase/functions/_shared/voice/context";
import { learnerWithSpeakingTask, makePremium, pause, recordedAnswer, service, setSetting, tutorReply, tutorSession, voice } from "./helpers/voice";

setDefaultTimeout(60_000);
beforeAll(async () => { await seedLocalContent(); });

describe("voice limits (parle-voice-service)", () => {
  it("a Free learner gets 2 recorded answers a day; the third is refused with the limit and the plan", async () => {
    const { t, taskId } = await learnerWithSpeakingTask();
    expect((await recordedAnswer(t, taskId)).take.status).toBe(200);
    expect((await recordedAnswer(t, taskId)).take.status).toBe(200);
    const third = await recordedAnswer(t, taskId);
    expect(third.take.status).toBe(429);
    expect(third.take.body).toMatchObject({ error_code: "LIMIT_REACHED", kind: "answer", plan: "free", limit: 2 });
    expect((await voice(t, { action: "voice_status" })).body.answers).toEqual({ limit: 2, used: 2, remaining: 0 });
  });
  it("a Premium learner gets the Premium limits", async () => {
    const { u, t } = await onboardedLearner();
    await makePremium(u.id);
    const s = (await voice(t, { action: "voice_status" })).body;
    expect([s.plan, s.answers.limit, s.messages.limit, s.replies.limit]).toEqual(["premium", 15, 60, 200]);
  });
  it("an administrator's new limit applies at once", async () => {
    const undo = await setSetting("free_voice_messages_per_day", 1);
    try {
      const { u, t } = await onboardedLearner();
      const session = await tutorSession(u.id, u.email);
      expect((await voice(t, { action: "voice_take", kind: "message", ref: session }, true)).status).toBe(200);
      const second = await voice(t, { action: "voice_take", kind: "message", ref: session }, true);
      expect([second.status, second.body.limit]).toEqual([429, 1]);
    } finally {
      await undo();
    }
  });
  it("a voice message that heard nothing gives its use back (Review Focus 1)", async () => {
    const { u, t } = await onboardedLearner();
    const session = await tutorSession(u.id, u.email);
    const take = await voice(t, { action: "voice_take", kind: "message", ref: session }, true);
    const part = await voice(t, { action: "voice_part", ticket_id: take.body.ticket_id, part: 1, transcript: "Sous-titres réalisés par la communauté d'Amara.org", duration_ms: 4000 }, true);
    expect([part.status, part.body.transcript, part.body.words]).toEqual([200, "", 0]);
    expect((await voice(t, { action: "voice_status" })).body.messages.used).toBe(0);
  });
  it("a reply read again or in pieces counts once; text that is not in the learner's own reply is refused (Review Focus 5)", async () => {
    const { u, t } = await onboardedLearner();
    const id = await tutorReply(u.id, u.email, "Très bien ! Here is a small correction.\nJe suis allé au marché. Nous avons acheté du pain.");
    const first = await voice(t, { action: "voice_take", kind: "reply", ref: id, text: "Très bien ! Je suis allé au marché." }, true);
    expect(first.status).toBe(200);
    const again = await voice(t, { action: "voice_take", kind: "reply", ref: id, text: "Nous avons acheté du pain." }, true);
    expect(again.body).toMatchObject({ again: true, used: 1 });
    const foreign = await voice(t, { action: "voice_take", kind: "reply", ref: id, text: "Bonjour, je vends des voitures pas chères." }, true);
    expect([foreign.status, foreign.body.error_code]).toEqual([403, "TEXT_NOT_IN_REPLY"]);
    const other = await onboardedLearner();
    expect((await voice(other.t, { action: "voice_take", kind: "reply", ref: id, text: "Je suis allé au marché." }, true)).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await voice(t, { action: "voice_status" })).body.replies.used).toBe(1);
  });
  it("only the relay may take units or save transcripts", async () => {
    const { t, taskId } = await learnerWithSpeakingTask();
    const r = await voice(t, { action: "voice_take", kind: "answer", ref: taskId });
    expect([r.status, r.body.error_code]).toEqual([403, "RELAY_ONLY"]);
  });
  it("a bad token and a paused account are refused", async () => {
    expect((await voice("not-a-token", { action: "voice_status" }, true)).status).toBe(401);
    const { u, t } = await onboardedLearner();
    expect((await voice(t, { action: "voice_status" })).status).toBe(200);
    await pause(u.id);
    expect((await voice(t, { action: "voice_take", kind: "message", ref: "x" }, true)).status).toBe(401);
  });
  it("parts arrive in order, at most four, and none after the answer is closed (Review Focus 4)", async () => {
    const { t, taskId } = await learnerWithSpeakingTask();
    const { ticketId } = await recordedAnswer(t, taskId, "Première partie de ma réponse sur mon travail.", 60_000);
    const take = (n: number, ticket = ticketId, token = t) => voice(token, { action: "voice_take", kind: "answer", ref: taskId, part: n, ticket_id: ticket }, true);
    const part = (n: number, ms: number) => voice(t, { action: "voice_part", ticket_id: ticketId, part: n, transcript: `Partie ${n} de ma réponse.`, duration_ms: ms }, true);
    expect((await take(3)).status).toBe(409);
    expect((await take(2)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await part(3, 10_000)).status).toBe(409);
    expect((await part(2, 60_000)).body.total_ms).toBe(120_000);
    expect((await take(3)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await part(3, 60_000)).body.total_ms).toBe(180_000);
    expect((await take(4)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await part(4, 3_000)).body.total_ms).toBe(183_000);
    expect((await take(5)).status).toBe(409);
    const other = await onboardedLearner();
    expect((await take(2, ticketId, other.t)).status).toBe(409);
    const second = await recordedAnswer(t, taskId);
    await voice(t, { action: "voice_release", ticket_id: second.ticketId }, true);
    expect((await take(2, second.ticketId)).status).toBe(409);
  });
  it("a use just before and just after the learner's local midnight counts on two days (Review Focus 3)", async () => {
    const { u } = await onboardedLearner(); // America/Toronto: UTC-4 in October
    const before = await learnerDay(service(), u.id, new Date("2026-10-17T03:59:00Z"));
    const after = await learnerDay(service(), u.id, new Date("2026-10-17T04:01:00Z"));
    expect([before.day, after.day, before.zone]).toEqual(["2026-10-16", "2026-10-17", "America/Toronto"]);
    for (const day of [before.day, after.day]) {
      const { data } = await service().rpc("voice_take", { p_user: u.id, p_kind: "answer", p_day: day, p_limit: 1, p_ref: "" });
      expect(data.ok).toBe(true);
    }
  });
  it("the read-aloud switch is off by default and remembered", async () => {
    const { t } = await onboardedLearner();
    expect((await voice(t, { action: "voice_status" })).body.read_aloud).toBe(false);
    expect((await voice(t, { action: "voice_set_read_aloud", read_aloud: true })).body.read_aloud).toBe(true);
    expect((await voice(t, { action: "voice_status" })).body.read_aloud).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 8: The voice context.** Create `parle-backend/supabase/functions/_shared/voice/context.ts` (editor tool):

```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";
import type { Caller } from "../caller.ts";
import { APP_SETTING_DEFAULTS, APP_SETTING_RULES, type AppSettingKey } from "../../_app/functions/parle-admin-settings.helper.ts";

export type VoiceKind = "answer" | "message" | "reply" | "placement";
export type Plan = "free" | "premium";
/** relay: the request carried the relay's shared secret (only the relay may take units and save transcripts). */
export type VoiceContext = { db: SupabaseClient; caller: Caller; body: Record<string, any>; now: Date; relay: boolean };

/** A refusal the app or the relay can act on: a code, an HTTP status, a plain message and details (limit, plan). */
export class VoiceError extends Error {
  code: string;
  status: number;
  extra: Record<string, unknown>;
  constructor(code: string, status: number, message = code, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "VoiceError";
    this.code = code;
    this.status = status;
    this.extra = extra;
  }
}

/** Placement speaking is never counted (spec 6) but a run may record at most this many times (ruling 6). */
export const PLACEMENT_ATTEMPTS = 6;
/** A 3-minute answer is sent in parts of at most 60 seconds (ruling 2). */
export const MAX_PARTS = 4;

const LIMIT_KEYS: Record<"answer" | "message" | "reply", readonly [AppSettingKey, AppSettingKey]> = {
  answer: ["free_voice_answers_per_day", "premium_voice_answers_per_day"],
  message: ["free_voice_messages_per_day", "premium_voice_messages_per_day"],
  reply: ["free_voice_replies_per_day", "premium_voice_replies_per_day"],
};

/** The administrator's limits (newest saved row per key), or the defaults; Premium is never below Free. */
export async function voiceLimits(db: SupabaseClient): Promise<Record<"answer" | "message" | "reply", { free: number; premium: number }>> {
  const keys = Object.values(LIMIT_KEYS).flat();
  const { data, error } = await db.from("app_setting").select("key, value, updated_at").in("key", keys).order("updated_at", { ascending: false });
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
  const read = (key: AppSettingKey): number => {
    const row = (data ?? []).find((r: { key: string }) => r.key === key);
    let value: unknown = row?.value;
    try { value = typeof value === "string" ? JSON.parse(value) : value; } catch { value = undefined; }
    const rule = APP_SETTING_RULES[key];
    return typeof value === "number" && Number.isInteger(value) && value >= Number(rule.min) && value <= Number(rule.max) ? value : Number(APP_SETTING_DEFAULTS[key]);
  };
  const out = {} as Record<"answer" | "message" | "reply", { free: number; premium: number }>;
  for (const [kind, [free, premium]] of Object.entries(LIMIT_KEYS) as Array<["answer" | "message" | "reply", readonly [AppSettingKey, AppSettingKey]]>) {
    const f = read(free);
    out[kind] = { free: f, premium: Math.max(f, read(premium)) };
  }
  return out;
}

/** Premium the way the admin user detail reads it (Plan 8, ruling 8): a live Stripe subscription or the comp flag. */
export async function planOf(db: SupabaseClient, userId: string): Promise<Plan> {
  const [subs, identity] = await Promise.all([
    db.from("Subscriber").select("stripe_status").eq("user_id", userId).limit(5),
    db.from("ParleIdentity").select("premium_comp_active").eq("owner_id", userId).limit(1),
  ]);
  if (subs.error) throw new Error(subs.error.message);
  if (identity.error) throw new Error(identity.error.message);
  const paid = (subs.data ?? []).some((s: { stripe_status?: unknown }) => ["active", "trialing", "past_due"].includes(String(s.stripe_status)));
  return paid || identity.data?.[0]?.premium_comp_active === true ? "premium" : "free";
}

function validZone(zone: unknown): zone is string {
  if (typeof zone !== "string" || !zone.trim()) return false;
  try { new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en", { timeZone: zone.trim() }); return true; } catch { return false; }
}

/** The learner's day, in the time zone the AI tutor uses: Parle profile, then learner profile, then UTC (ruling 9). */
export async function learnerDay(db: SupabaseClient, userId: string, now: Date): Promise<{ day: string; zone: string }> {
  const [parle, learner] = await Promise.all([
    db.from("ParleProfile").select("time_zone").eq("owner_id", userId).order("created_at", { ascending: false }).limit(1),
    db.from("learner_profile").select("time_zone").eq("user_id", userId).order("updated_at", { ascending: false }).limit(1),
  ]);
  const zone = [parle.data?.[0]?.time_zone, learner.data?.[0]?.time_zone].find(validZone)?.trim() ?? "UTC";
  const day = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { timeZone: zone, year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit" }).format(now);
  return { day, zone };
}

/** Corrections and tips are written in the learner's explanation language (spec 4.2), else English. */
export async function explanationLanguage(db: SupabaseClient, userId: string): Promise<string> {
  const { data } = await db.from("ParleProfile").select("native_language, explain_in_native").eq("owner_id", userId).order("created_at", { ascending: false }).limit(1);
  const p = data?.[0];
  return p?.explain_in_native === true && typeof p.native_language === "string" && p.native_language.trim() ? p.native_language.trim().slice(0, 60) : "English";
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Limits, parts, status.** Create `parle-backend/supabase/functions/_shared/voice/limits.ts` (editor tool):

```ts
import { cleanTranscript, countSpokenWords, MAX_SPEAK_CHARS, spokenTextIsFrom } from "../../_app/src/lib/voice-text.ts";
import { takeRateLimit } from "../rate-limit.ts";
import { learnerDay, MAX_PARTS, PLACEMENT_ATTEMPTS, planOf, voiceLimits, VoiceError, type VoiceContext, type VoiceKind } from "./context.ts";

const KINDS: VoiceKind[] = ["answer", "message", "reply", "placement"];
export const SPEAKING_TASK_TYPES = ["speaking_practice", "speaking_check"];
const OPEN = ["planned", "rolled"];
const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
const PART_MS = 61_000; // 60 s parts, with a second of slack
const TOTAL_MS = 183_000; // 3 minutes (spec 3), with slack
const PART_REFUSED = () => new VoiceError("PART_REFUSED", 409, "This recording can no longer be added to. Record your answer again.");

function first<T = Record<string, any>>(r: { data: T[] | null; error: { message: string } | null }): T | null {
  if (r.error) throw new Error(r.error.message);
  return r.data?.[0] ?? null;
}

/** The caller's own speaking task (any status), or null. */
export async function ownSpeakingTask(ctx: VoiceContext, taskId: string): Promise<Record<string, any> | null> {
  if (!UUID.test(taskId)) return null;
  const task = first(await ctx.db.from("learner_task").select("id, type, status, roadmap_id, content_json, topic, owner_id").eq("id", taskId).eq("owner_id", ctx.caller.id).limit(1));
  return task && SPEAKING_TASK_TYPES.includes(String(task.type)) ? task : null;
}

export async function ownTicket(ctx: VoiceContext, id: string): Promise<Record<string, any> | null> {
  if (!UUID.test(id)) return null;
  return first(await ctx.db.from("voice_use").select("*").eq("id", id).eq("user_id", ctx.caller.id).limit(1));
}

/** What the use is for must be the caller's own: an open speaking task, an open placement run, a tutor session, a tutor reply. */
async function checkRef(ctx: VoiceContext, kind: VoiceKind, ref: string, text: string): Promise<void> {
  if (kind === "answer") {
    const task = await ownSpeakingTask(ctx, ref);
    if (!task) throw new VoiceError("TASK_NOT_FOUND", 404, "This speaking task was not found.");
    if (!OPEN.includes(String(task.status))) throw new VoiceError("TASK_CLOSED", 409, "This task is already done.");
    return;
  }
  if (kind === "placement") {
    const run = UUID.test(ref) ? first(await ctx.db.from("placement_run").select("id").eq("id", ref).eq("owner_id", ctx.caller.id).eq("status", "open").limit(1)) : null;
    if (!run) throw new VoiceError("RUN_NOT_FOUND", 404, "This placement test was not found.");
    return;
  }
  if (kind === "message") {
    const session = UUID.test(ref) ? first(await ctx.db.from("ai_session").select("id").eq("id", ref).eq("owner_id", ctx.caller.id).limit(1)) : null;
    if (!session) throw new VoiceError("SESSION_NOT_FOUND", 404, "This tutor session was not found.");
    return;
  }
  if (!text || text.length > MAX_SPEAK_CHARS + 100) throw new VoiceError("INVALID_INPUT", 400, "Choose a shorter part of the reply.");
  const reply = UUID.test(ref) ? first(await ctx.db.from("ai_message").select("text, sender").eq("id", ref).eq("owner_id", ctx.caller.id).limit(1)) : null;
  if (!reply || reply.sender !== "tutor_ai") throw new VoiceError("REPLY_NOT_FOUND", 404, "This reply was not found.");
  if (!spokenTextIsFrom(text, String(reply.text ?? ""))) throw new VoiceError("TEXT_NOT_IN_REPLY", 403, "Only the tutor's own reply can be read aloud.");
}

/** A later part of an answer adds to the same ticket: in order, within 15 minutes, at most MAX_PARTS. No new count. */
async function continueAnswer(ctx: VoiceContext, kind: VoiceKind, ticketId: string, part: number) {
  const t = await ownTicket(ctx, ticketId);
  if (!t || t.kind !== kind || t.status !== "pending" || t.parts !== part - 1 || Date.parse(t.created_at) < ctx.now.getTime() - 15 * 60_000) throw PART_REFUSED();
  return { ticket_id: t.id, kind, plan: null, limit: null, used: null, remaining: null, again: true };
}

/** Relay: take one unit of the learner's daily limit before calling NVIDIA (spec 3). */
export async function voiceTake(ctx: VoiceContext): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  const kind = String(ctx.body.kind ?? "") as VoiceKind;
  if (!KINDS.includes(kind)) throw new VoiceError("INVALID_INPUT", 400);
  const ref = String(ctx.body.ref ?? "").slice(0, 200);
  const part = Number(ctx.body.part ?? 1);
  const multi = kind === "answer" || kind === "placement";
  if (!Number.isInteger(part) || part < 1 || part > (multi ? MAX_PARTS : 1)) throw PART_REFUSED();
  if (part > 1) return continueAnswer(ctx, kind, String(ctx.body.ticket_id ?? ""), part);
  await checkRef(ctx, kind, ref, typeof ctx.body.text === "string" ? ctx.body.text.trim() : "");
  const [plan, { day }, limits] = await Promise.all([planOf(ctx.db, ctx.caller.id), learnerDay(ctx.db, ctx.caller.id, ctx.now), voiceLimits(ctx.db)]);
  const limit = kind === "placement" ? PLACEMENT_ATTEMPTS : limits[kind][plan];
  const { data, error } = await ctx.db.rpc("voice_take", { p_user: ctx.caller.id, p_kind: kind, p_day: day, p_limit: limit, p_ref: ref });
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
  const r = data as { ok: boolean; ticket_id?: string; used: number; again?: boolean };
  if (!r.ok) {
    throw kind === "placement"
      ? new VoiceError("PLACEMENT_ATTEMPTS_USED", 429, "You have used the recordings for this placement test.", { kind, plan, limit, used: r.used })
      : new VoiceError("LIMIT_REACHED", 429, "Today's voice limit is reached.", { kind, plan, limit, used: r.used });
  }
  // replaying a reply is free, but not unlimited: the relay must not become free text-to-speech (Review Focus 5)
  if (r.again && !(await takeRateLimit(ctx.db, `voice:again:${ctx.caller.id}`, 3600, 100))) throw new VoiceError("RATE_LIMITED", 429, "Try again later.", { kind });
  return { ticket_id: r.ticket_id, kind, plan, limit, used: r.used, remaining: kind === "placement" ? null : Math.max(0, limit - r.used), again: r.again === true };
}

/** Relay: save what Whisper heard. Messages keep nothing (the app shows the text); answers add the part in order. */
export async function voicePart(ctx: VoiceContext): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  const t = await ownTicket(ctx, String(ctx.body.ticket_id ?? ""));
  if (!t) throw new VoiceError("TICKET_NOT_FOUND", 404, "This recording was not found.");
  const part = Number(ctx.body.part ?? 1);
  const durationMs = Math.round(Number(ctx.body.duration_ms ?? 0));
  if (!Number.isFinite(durationMs) || durationMs < 0 || durationMs > PART_MS) throw new VoiceError("PART_TOO_LONG", 400, "A part may be at most 60 seconds.");
  const text = cleanTranscript(typeof ctx.body.transcript === "string" ? ctx.body.transcript.slice(0, 6000) : "");
  if (t.kind === "message") {
    if (!text) {
      const { error } = await ctx.db.from("voice_use").update({ status: "void", void_reason: "empty", settled_at: ctx.now.toISOString() }).eq("id", t.id).eq("status", "counted");
      if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
    }
    return { transcript: text, words: countSpokenWords(text), duration_ms: durationMs, total_ms: durationMs };
  }
  if (t.kind !== "answer" && t.kind !== "placement") throw new VoiceError("TICKET_NOT_FOUND", 404, "This recording was not found.");
  if (t.status !== "pending" || t.parts !== part - 1) throw PART_REFUSED();
  const total = Number(t.duration_ms) + durationMs;
  if (total > TOTAL_MS) throw new VoiceError("ANSWER_TOO_LONG", 400, "An answer may be at most 3 minutes.");
  const transcript = [t.transcript, text].filter(Boolean).join(" ").slice(0, 6000);
  const { data, error } = await ctx.db.from("voice_use").update({ parts: part, transcript, duration_ms: total }).eq("id", t.id).eq("status", "pending").eq("parts", part - 1).select("id");
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
  if (!data?.length) throw PART_REFUSED();
  return { transcript: text, words: countSpokenWords(transcript), duration_ms: durationMs, total_ms: total };
}

/** Relay: the provider failed, so the use is given back (a reply's row is removed so it can be read later). */
export async function voiceRelease(ctx: VoiceContext): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  const t = await ownTicket(ctx, String(ctx.body.ticket_id ?? ""));
  if (!t || Date.parse(t.created_at) < ctx.now.getTime() - 10 * 60_000) return { released: false };
  if (t.kind === "reply") {
    const { error } = await ctx.db.from("voice_use").delete().eq("id", t.id);
    if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
    return { released: true };
  }
  const { data, error } = await ctx.db.from("voice_use").update({ status: "void", void_reason: "provider_failed", settled_at: ctx.now.toISOString() }).eq("id", t.id).in("status", ["pending", "counted"]).select("id");
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
  return { released: Boolean(data?.length) };
}

/** Learner: today's limits and uses, and the read-aloud switch. */
export async function voiceStatus(ctx: VoiceContext): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  const [plan, { day, zone }, limits, pref] = await Promise.all([
    planOf(ctx.db, ctx.caller.id),
    learnerDay(ctx.db, ctx.caller.id, ctx.now),
    voiceLimits(ctx.db),
    ctx.db.from("voice_preference").select("read_aloud").eq("user_id", ctx.caller.id).maybeSingle(),
  ]);
  const { data, error } = await ctx.db.from("voice_use").select("kind").eq("user_id", ctx.caller.id).eq("day", day).neq("status", "void");
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
  const entry = (kind: "answer" | "message" | "reply") => {
    const limit = limits[kind][plan];
    const used = (data ?? []).filter((r: { kind: string }) => r.kind === kind).length;
    return { limit, used, remaining: Math.max(0, limit - used) };
  };
  return { plan, day, time_zone: zone, answers: entry("answer"), messages: entry("message"), replies: entry("reply"), read_aloud: pref.data?.read_aloud === true };
}

/** Learner: "Read replies aloud", off by default, remembered per learner (spec 5). */
export async function setReadAloud(ctx: VoiceContext): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  const on = ctx.body.read_aloud === true;
  const { error } = await ctx.db.from("voice_preference").upsert({ user_id: ctx.caller.id, read_aloud: on, updated_at: ctx.now.toISOString() });
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
  return { read_aloud: on };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Router and function.** Create `parle-backend/supabase/functions/_shared/voice/actions.ts` (editor tool):

```ts
import { VoiceError, type VoiceContext } from "./context.ts";
import { setReadAloud, voicePart, voiceRelease, voiceStatus, voiceTake } from "./limits.ts";

type Action = (ctx: VoiceContext) => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>;
/** Called only by the relay (header x-voice-secret), always with the learner's own token. */
const RELAY: Record<string, Action> = { voice_take: voiceTake, voice_part: voicePart, voice_release: voiceRelease };
/** Called by the app with the learner's token. */
const LEARNER: Record<string, Action> = { voice_status: voiceStatus, voice_set_read_aloud: setReadAloud };

export async function runVoiceAction(ctx: VoiceContext): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  const action = String(ctx.body.action ?? "");
  if (RELAY[action]) {
    if (!ctx.relay) throw new VoiceError("RELAY_ONLY", 403, "Only the voice relay may do this.");
    return RELAY[action](ctx);
  }
  if (LEARNER[action]) return LEARNER[action](ctx);
  throw new VoiceError("UNKNOWN_ACTION", 400);
}
```

  Create `parle-backend/supabase/functions/parle-voice-service/index.ts` (editor tool):

```ts
import { serviceClient } from "../_shared/db.ts";
import { getCaller } from "../_shared/caller.ts";
import { fail, json, preflight } from "../_shared/http.ts";
import { serveWith } from "../_shared/serve.ts";
import { aiActionLimit } from "../_shared/guards.ts";
import { sameSecret } from "../_shared/secret.ts";
import { VoiceError } from "../_shared/voice/context.ts";
import { runVoiceAction } from "../_shared/voice/actions.ts";

/**
 * Plan 9: voice limits, transcripts, speaking scores and listening audio. The relay (Vercel) calls with the
 * learner's token and the shared secret; the app calls with the learner's token. getCaller() refuses bad tokens,
 * unconfirmed emails and paused accounts, so the relay never reaches NVIDIA for them.
 */
serveWith(async (req) => {
  const pre = preflight(req);
  if (pre) return pre;
  const db = serviceClient();
  const caller = await getCaller(req, db);
  if (!caller) return fail("AUTH_REQUIRED", 401, "Authentication required");
  let body: Record<string, any>;
  try { body = await req.json(); } catch { return fail("INVALID_INPUT", 400); }
  const relay = sameSecret(req.headers.get("x-voice-secret") ?? "", Deno.env.get("VOICE_RELAY_SECRET") ?? "");
  try {
    return json({ status: "complete", ...(await runVoiceAction({ db, caller, body, now: new Date(), relay })) });
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof VoiceError) return json({ error: e.code, error_code: e.code, message: e.message, ...e.extra }, e.status);
    console.error("parle-voice-service", body.action, (e as Error).message);
    return fail("REQUEST_FAILED", 500, "Something went wrong. Please try again.");
  }
}, aiActionLimit);
```
