- [ ] **Step 3: Pure audio.** Create `parle-app/src/lib/voice-audio.ts` (editor tool):

```ts
/** Plan 9: preparing a recording in the browser (ruling 1). Pure functions, tested without a browser. */
export const UPLOAD_RATE = 16000;
export const PART_SECONDS = 60; // ruling 2: Vercel's 4.5 MB body limit
export const ANSWER_MAX_MS = 180_000; // spec 3: recordings up to 3 minutes
export const MESSAGE_MAX_MS = 60_000; // spec 5: a tutor voice message records up to 1 minute

const RECORDING_TYPES = ["audio/webm;codecs=opus", "audio/webm", "audio/mp4;codecs=mp4a.40.2", "audio/mp4", "audio/ogg;codecs=opus"];
/** Chrome, Firefox and Edge record WebM/Opus; Safari and iPhone record MP4/AAC; "" lets the browser choose. */
export function pickRecordingType(isSupported: (type: string) => boolean): string {
  return RECORDING_TYPES.find((type) => { try { return isSupported(type); } catch { return false; } }) ?? "";
}

export function mixToMono(channels: Float32Array[]): Float32Array {
  if (channels.length === 1) return channels[0].slice();
  const out = new Float32Array(channels[0]?.length ?? 0);
  for (const channel of channels) for (let i = 0; i < out.length; i++) out[i] += channel[i] / channels.length;
  return out;
}

/** Averaging resampler: each output sample is the mean of the input samples it covers (a simple low-pass). */
export function resample(input: Float32Array, from: number, to = UPLOAD_RATE): Float32Array {
  if (from === to) return input.slice();
  const ratio = from / to;
  const out = new Float32Array(Math.floor(input.length / ratio));
  for (let i = 0; i < out.length; i++) {
    const start = Math.floor(i * ratio);
    const end = Math.min(input.length, Math.max(start + 1, Math.floor((i + 1) * ratio)));
    let sum = 0;
    for (let j = start; j < end; j++) sum += input[j];
    out[i] = sum / (end - start);
  }
  return out;
}

export function encodeWav16(samples: Float32Array, rate = UPLOAD_RATE): Uint8Array {
  const out = new Uint8Array(44 + samples.length * 2);
  const view = new DataView(out.buffer);
  const text = (o: number, s: string) => { for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) out[o + i] = s.charCodeAt(i); };
  text(0, "RIFF"); view.setUint32(4, 36 + samples.length * 2, true); text(8, "WAVE"); text(12, "fmt ");
  view.setUint32(16, 16, true); view.setUint16(20, 1, true); view.setUint16(22, 1, true); view.setUint32(24, rate, true);
  view.setUint32(28, rate * 2, true); view.setUint16(32, 2, true); view.setUint16(34, 16, true); text(36, "data");
  view.setUint32(40, samples.length * 2, true);
  for (let i = 0; i < samples.length; i++) {
    const s = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, samples[i]));
    view.setInt16(44 + i * 2, s < 0 ? Math.round(s * 32768) : Math.round(s * 32767), true);
  }
  return out;
}

/** Parts of at most `maxSeconds`, each cut at the quietest 100 ms of its last 10 seconds (ruling 2). */
export function splitForUpload(samples: Float32Array, rate = UPLOAD_RATE, maxSeconds = PART_SECONDS): Float32Array[] {
  const max = rate * maxSeconds, win = Math.round(rate * 0.1);
  const parts: Float32Array[] = [];
  let start = 0;
  while (samples.length - start > max) {
    let best = start + max, quietest = Infinity;
    for (let s = start + max - rate * 10; s + win <= start + max; s += win) {
      let energy = 0;
      for (let j = s; j < s + win; j++) energy += samples[j] * samples[j];
      if (energy < quietest) { quietest = energy; best = s + Math.floor(win / 2); }
    }
    parts.push(samples.subarray(start, best));
    start = best;
  }
  parts.push(samples.subarray(start));
  return parts;
}

/** Less than half a second above a quiet room's level: nothing is sent (Review Focus 1). */
export function isSilent(samples: Float32Array, rate = UPLOAD_RATE): boolean {
  const win = Math.round(rate * 0.1);
  let voiced = 0;
  for (let s = 0; s + win <= samples.length; s += win) {
    let energy = 0;
    for (let j = s; j < s + win; j++) energy += samples[j] * samples[j];
    if (Math.sqrt(energy / win) > 0.02) voiced++;
  }
  return voiced < 5;
}

export function formatClock(ms: number): string {
  const total = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 1000));
  return `${Math.floor(total / 60)}:${String(total % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}
```

  Create `parle-app/src/lib/voice-recorder.ts` (editor tool):

```ts
import { mixToMono, pickRecordingType, resample, UPLOAD_RATE } from "./voice-audio";

/** The microphone is asked for only when Record is pressed (spec 5). */
export class MicrophoneError extends Error {
  code: "denied" | "unsupported" | "no_device";
  constructor(code: "denied" | "unsupported" | "no_device") {
    super(code);
    this.code = code;
  }
}
export type Recording = { stop(): Promise<Blob>; cancel(): void };

export async function startRecording(maxMs: number, onTick: (ms: number) => void, onAutoStop: () => void): Promise<Recording> {
  if (typeof navigator === "undefined" || !navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia || typeof MediaRecorder === "undefined") throw new MicrophoneError("unsupported");
  let stream: MediaStream;
  try {
    stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: { channelCount: 1, echoCancellation: true, noiseSuppression: true } });
  } catch (error) {
    const name = (error as { name?: string })?.name ?? "";
    throw new MicrophoneError(name === "NotAllowedError" || name === "SecurityError" ? "denied" : name === "NotFoundError" ? "no_device" : "unsupported");
  }
  const type = pickRecordingType((t) => MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(t));
  const recorder = new MediaRecorder(stream, type ? { mimeType: type, audioBitsPerSecond: 64000 } : undefined);
  const chunks: Blob[] = [];
  recorder.ondataavailable = (event) => { if (event.data.size) chunks.push(event.data); };
  const started = Date.now();
  const timer = setInterval(() => {
    const ms = Date.now() - started;
    onTick(Math.min(ms, maxMs));
    if (ms >= maxMs) onAutoStop();
  }, 250);
  const finished = new Promise<Blob>((resolve) => {
    recorder.onstop = () => {
      clearInterval(timer);
      stream.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
      resolve(new Blob(chunks, { type: recorder.mimeType || type || "audio/webm" }));
    };
  });
  recorder.start(1000);
  const halt = () => { if (recorder.state !== "inactive") recorder.stop(); };
  return { stop: () => { halt(); return finished; }, cancel: halt };
}

/** Decodes the browser's own recording (WebM/Opus or MP4/AAC) to 16 kHz mono samples. */
export async function blobToSamples(blob: Blob): Promise<Float32Array> {
  const Context = window.AudioContext ?? (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
  const context = new Context();
  try {
    const audio = await context.decodeAudioData(await blob.arrayBuffer());
    const channels = Array.from({ length: audio.numberOfChannels }, (_, i) => audio.getChannelData(i));
    return resample(mixToMono(channels), audio.sampleRate, UPLOAD_RATE);
  } finally {
    void context.close();
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The client.** In `parle-app/src/functions/index.ts` add the line
  `export const parleVoiceService = functions.parleVoiceService;`. Create `parle-app/src/lib/voice-client.ts` (editor tool):

```ts
import { parleVoiceService } from "@/functions";
import type { TranslationKey } from "@/lib/i18n";
import type { SpeakingAssessment } from "./placement-test";
import { encodeWav16, splitForUpload, UPLOAD_RATE } from "./voice-audio";
import { countSpokenWords } from "./voice-text";

/** Plan 9: calls to the voice relay (/api/voice) and to parle-voice-service. */
export class VoiceRequestError extends Error {
  code: string;
  info: { limit?: number; plan?: "free" | "premium" };
  constructor(code: string, info: { limit?: number; plan?: "free" | "premium" } = {}) {
    super(code);
    this.name = "VoiceRequestError";
    this.code = code;
    this.info = info;
  }
}
type Deps = { token: () => Promise<string | null>; fetch: (input: string, init: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>; wait: (ms: number) => Promise<void> };
const defaults: Deps = {
  token: async () => {
    const { supabase } = await import("@/lib/backend/client");
    return (await supabase.auth.getSession()).data.session?.access_token ?? null;
  },
  fetch: (input, init) => fetch(input, init),
  wait: (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms)),
};
let deps: Deps = defaults;
/** Tests replace the token, fetch and wait; null restores the defaults. */
export function setVoiceDeps(next: Partial<Deps> | null) { deps = next ? { ...defaults, ...next } : defaults; }

const isObject = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, any> => Boolean(v) && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v);
const infoOf = (b: Record<string, unknown>) => ({ limit: typeof b.limit === "number" ? b.limit : undefined, plan: b.plan === "premium" || b.plan === "free" ? (b.plan as "free" | "premium") : undefined });
/** Errors thrown by the functions proxy carry the answer body. */
export function voiceErrorFrom(error: unknown): VoiceRequestError {
  if (error instanceof VoiceRequestError) return error;
  const body = isObject((error as { body?: unknown })?.body) ? (error as { body: Record<string, unknown> }).body : {};
  return new VoiceRequestError(String(body.error_code ?? "REQUEST_FAILED"), infoOf(body));
}

async function relay(query: URLSearchParams, init: RequestInit): Promise<Response> {
  const token = await deps.token();
  if (!token) throw new VoiceRequestError("AUTH_REQUIRED");
  const r = await deps.fetch(`/api/voice?${query.toString()}`, { ...init, headers: { ...((init.headers ?? {}) as Record<string, string>), Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }).catch(() => null);
  if (!r) throw new VoiceRequestError("VOICE_BUSY");
  if (!r.ok) {
    const body = (await r.json().catch(() => ({}))) as Record<string, unknown>;
    throw new VoiceRequestError(String(body.error_code ?? (r.status === 429 ? "LIMIT_REACHED" : "VOICE_BUSY")), infoOf(body));
  }
  return r;
}

export type Transcription = { ticketId: string; transcript: string; words: number; durationMs: number; remaining: number | null };
export async function transcribe(samples: Float32Array, purpose: "answer" | "message" | "placement", ref: string): Promise<Transcription> {
  const parts = purpose === "message" ? [samples] : splitForUpload(samples, UPLOAD_RATE);
  let ticketId = "", transcript = "", durationMs = 0;
  let remaining: number | null = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
    const query = new URLSearchParams({ action: "transcribe", purpose, ref, part: String(i + 1) });
    if (ticketId) query.set("ticket", ticketId);
    const r = await relay(query, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "audio/wav" }, body: encodeWav16(parts[i], UPLOAD_RATE) });
    const body = (await r.json()) as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (i === 0) { ticketId = String(body.ticket_id ?? ""); remaining = typeof body.remaining === "number" ? body.remaining : null; }
    transcript = [transcript, String(body.transcript ?? "")].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
    durationMs += Number(body.duration_ms ?? 0);
  }
  return { ticketId, transcript, words: countSpokenWords(transcript), durationMs, remaining };
}

export async function speakChunk(text: string, replyId: string): Promise<Blob> {
  const r = await relay(new URLSearchParams({ action: "speak" }), { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, reply_id: replyId }) });
  return r.blob();
}

/** A playable link for a listening item; waits while another request is making the audio. */
export async function listeningUrl(kind: "question_item" | "learner_task", id: string): Promise<string> {
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 6; attempt++) {
    try {
      const r = await relay(new URLSearchParams({ action: "listen" }), { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ item_kind: kind, item_id: id }) });
      return String(((await r.json()) as Record<string, unknown>).url ?? "");
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof VoiceRequestError && error.code === "AUDIO_BEING_MADE" && attempt < 5) { await deps.wait(3000); continue; }
      throw error;
    }
  }
  throw new VoiceRequestError("VOICE_BUSY");
}

async function service(action: string, body: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Promise<Record<string, any>> {
  try {
    const r = await parleVoiceService({ action, ...body });
    return isObject(r) ? r : {};
  } catch (error) {
    throw voiceErrorFrom(error);
  }
}

export type VoiceAllowance = { limit: number; used: number; remaining: number };
export type VoiceStatus = { plan: "free" | "premium"; answers: VoiceAllowance; messages: VoiceAllowance; replies: VoiceAllowance; readAloud: boolean };
const allowance = (v: unknown): VoiceAllowance => ({ limit: Number(isObject(v) ? v.limit : 0) || 0, used: Number(isObject(v) ? v.used : 0) || 0, remaining: Number(isObject(v) ? v.remaining : 0) || 0 });
export async function loadVoiceStatus(): Promise<VoiceStatus> {
  const s = await service("voice_status");
  return { plan: s.plan === "premium" ? "premium" : "free", answers: allowance(s.answers), messages: allowance(s.messages), replies: allowance(s.replies), readAloud: s.read_aloud === true };
}
export async function saveReadAloud(on: boolean): Promise<boolean> {
  return (await service("voice_set_read_aloud", { read_aloud: on })).read_aloud === true;
}

export type SpeakingCorrection = { said: string; better: string; reason: string };
export type SpeakingResult = {
  id: string; mode: "spoken" | "typed"; transcript: string;
  criteria: { task_completion: number; vocabulary: number; grammar: number; coherence_fluency: number };
  level: string; nclc: string; corrections: SpeakingCorrection[]; tip: string; wordsPerMinute: number | null; durationMs: number;
};
const LEVELS = ["A1", "A2", "B1", "B2", "C1"];
const str = (v: unknown, max = 2000) => (typeof v === "string" ? v.slice(0, max) : "");
export function safeSpeakingResult(v: unknown): SpeakingResult | null {
  if (!isObject(v) || !LEVELS.includes(String(v.level)) || !isObject(v.criteria)) return null;
  const c = v.criteria;
  const score = (n: unknown) => (Number.isInteger(n) && Number(n) >= 0 && Number(n) <= 4 ? Number(n) : null);
  const criteria = { task_completion: score(c.task_completion), vocabulary: score(c.vocabulary), grammar: score(c.grammar), coherence_fluency: score(c.coherence_fluency) };
  if (Object.values(criteria).some((n) => n === null)) return null;
  return {
    id: str(v.id, 64), mode: v.mode === "typed" ? "typed" : "spoken", transcript: str(v.transcript, 6000), criteria: criteria as SpeakingResult["criteria"],
    level: String(v.level), nclc: str(v.nclc, 40), tip: str(v.tip, 600),
    corrections: (Array.isArray(v.corrections) ? v.corrections : []).filter(isObject).slice(0, 3).map((x) => ({ said: str(x.said, 300), better: str(x.better, 300), reason: str(x.reason, 300) })),
    wordsPerMinute: typeof v.words_per_minute === "number" ? v.words_per_minute : null, durationMs: Number(v.duration_ms ?? 0) || 0,
  };
}
export type ScoreOutcome = { status: "scored"; result: SpeakingResult } | { status: "too_short" | "not_french" };
export function safeOutcome(v: unknown): ScoreOutcome {
  const o = isObject(v) ? v : {};
  if (o.status === "too_short" || o.status === "not_french") return { status: o.status };
  const result = o.status === "scored" ? safeSpeakingResult(o.result) : null;
  if (!result) throw new VoiceRequestError("SCORE_FAILED");
  return { status: "scored", result };
}
/** Scores a transcribed answer; a second press while it is being scored waits for the same result (Review Focus 2). */
export async function scoreSpokenAnswer(ticketId: string, taskId: string): Promise<ScoreOutcome> {
  for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
    try {
      return safeOutcome(await service("speaking_score", { ticket_id: ticketId, task_id: taskId }));
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof VoiceRequestError && error.code === "SCORING_IN_PROGRESS" && attempt < 5) { await deps.wait(3000); continue; }
      throw error;
    }
  }
}
export const scoreTypedAnswer = async (taskId: string, text: string) => safeOutcome(await service("speaking_score_typed", { task_id: taskId, text }));
export const loadTaskSpeakingResult = async (taskId: string) => safeSpeakingResult((await service("speaking_result", { task_id: taskId })).result);
export async function loadListeningMissing(): Promise<{ total: number; missing: Array<{ item_kind: "question_item" | "learner_task"; item_id: string }> }> {
  const r = await service("admin_listening_missing");
  const missing = (Array.isArray(r.missing) ? r.missing : []).filter(isObject).map((m) => ({ item_kind: m.item_kind === "learner_task" ? "learner_task" as const : "question_item" as const, item_id: str(m.item_id, 64) })).filter((m) => m.item_id);
  return { total: Number(r.total ?? missing.length) || 0, missing };
}
export function resultFromAssessment(a: SpeakingAssessment): SpeakingResult {
  return { id: `placement-${a.prompt_id}`, mode: "spoken", transcript: a.transcript, criteria: a.rubric, level: a.spoken_level, nclc: a.nclc, corrections: a.corrections, tip: a.tip, wordsPerMinute: a.words_per_minute, durationMs: 0 };
}

export function voiceErrorKey(error: unknown, kind: "answer" | "message" | "reply" | "listening"): TranslationKey {
  const code = error instanceof VoiceRequestError ? error.code : "";
  if (code === "LIMIT_REACHED") return kind === "message" ? "voice.limit.messages" : kind === "reply" ? "voice.limit.replies" : "voice.limit.answers";
  if (code === "PLACEMENT_ATTEMPTS_USED") return "voice.placementAttempts";
  if (code === "SCORE_FAILED") return "voice.scoreFailed";
  if (kind === "listening") return "voice.listening.unavailable";
  if (["VOICE_BUSY", "VOICE_NOT_CONFIGURED", "AUDIO_BEING_MADE", "RATE_LIMITED", "SCORING_IN_PROGRESS", "REQUEST_FAILED"].includes(code)) return "voice.busy";
  return "voice.failed";
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The components.** Create `parle-app/src/components/parle/VoiceRecorder.tsx` (editor tool):

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Keyboard, LoaderCircle, Mic, RotateCcw, Send, Square } from "lucide-react";
import { t, type TranslationKey } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { formatClock, isSilent } from "@/lib/voice-audio";
import { blobToSamples, MicrophoneError, startRecording, type Recording } from "@/lib/voice-recorder";
import { transcribe, voiceErrorKey, VoiceRequestError, type Transcription } from "@/lib/voice-client";

type Phase = "idle" | "asking" | "recording" | "preparing" | "recorded" | "sending";
type Problem = { key: TranslationKey; offerTyping: boolean; plan?: "free" | "premium"; limit?: number };

/** Record, a visible timer, Stop, Record again, Send (spec 4.1). Re-recording before sending is free (spec 4.3). */
export function VoiceRecorder({ purpose, refId, maxMs, disabled = false, onTranscribed, onTypeInstead }: {
  purpose: "answer" | "placement" | "message";
  refId: string;
  maxMs: number;
  disabled?: boolean;
  onTranscribed: (result: Transcription) => void;
  onTypeInstead?: () => void;
}) {
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<Phase>("idle");
  const [elapsed, setElapsed] = useState(0);
  const [samples, setSamples] = useState<Float32Array | null>(null);
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<Problem | null>(null);
  const [announcement, setAnnouncement] = useState("");
  const recording = useRef<Recording | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => () => recording.current?.cancel(), []);

  async function stop() {
    const current = recording.current;
    if (!current) return;
    recording.current = null;
    setPhase("preparing");
    const blob = await current.stop();
    setAnnouncement(t("voice.recorder.stopped"));
    try {
      const pcm = await blobToSamples(blob);
      if (isSilent(pcm)) { setPhase("idle"); setProblem({ key: "voice.tooQuiet", offerTyping: false }); return; }
      setSamples(pcm);
      setPhase("recorded");
    } catch {
      setPhase("idle");
      setProblem({ key: "voice.decodeFailed", offerTyping: true });
    }
  }

  async function start() {
    setProblem(null);
    setSamples(null);
    setElapsed(0);
    setPhase("asking");
    try {
      recording.current = await startRecording(maxMs, setElapsed, () => void stop());
      setPhase("recording");
      setAnnouncement(t("voice.recorder.started"));
    } catch (error) {
      setPhase("idle");
      const code = error instanceof MicrophoneError ? error.code : "unsupported";
      setProblem({ key: code === "denied" ? "voice.micDenied" : code === "no_device" ? "voice.micMissing" : "voice.micUnsupported", offerTyping: true });
    }
  }

  async function send() {
    if (!samples) return;
    setPhase("sending");
    setProblem(null);
    try {
      const result = await transcribe(samples, purpose, refId);
      setSamples(null);
      setPhase("idle");
      onTranscribed(result);
    } catch (error) {
      setPhase("recorded");
      const limited = error instanceof VoiceRequestError && error.code === "LIMIT_REACHED";
      setProblem({ key: voiceErrorKey(error, purpose === "message" ? "message" : "answer"), offerTyping: limited, plan: limited ? error.info.plan : undefined, limit: limited ? error.info.limit : undefined });
    }
  }

  const button = "inline-flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60";
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 space-y-3">
      {phase === "idle" && <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">{t("voice.recorder.permissionNote")}</p>}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        {(phase === "idle" || phase === "asking") && (
          <button type="button" onClick={() => void start()} disabled={disabled || phase === "asking"} aria-label={t("voice.recorder.recordLabel")} className={`${button} bg-primary text-white hover:bg-[#172E70]`}>
            {phase === "asking" ? <LoaderCircle aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Mic aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />}{t("voice.recorder.record")}
          </button>
        )}
        {phase === "recording" && (
          <button type="button" onClick={() => void stop()} className={`${button} bg-[#A13F36] text-white hover:bg-[#8D342D]`}>
            <Square aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />{t("voice.recorder.stop")}
          </button>
        )}
        {(phase === "recorded" || phase === "sending") && (
          <>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => void send()} disabled={disabled || phase === "sending"} className={`${button} bg-primary text-white hover:bg-[#172E70]`}>
              {phase === "sending" ? <LoaderCircle aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Send aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />}
              {t(phase === "sending" ? "voice.recorder.sending" : purpose === "message" ? "voice.recorder.useRecording" : "voice.recorder.send")}
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => void start()} disabled={phase === "sending"} className={`${button} border border-primary/15 bg-white text-primary hover:bg-[#F7F8FB]`}>
              <RotateCcw aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />{t("voice.recorder.rerecord")}
            </button>
          </>
        )}
        {phase === "preparing" && <span role="status" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground"><LoaderCircle aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />{t("voice.recorder.preparing")}</span>}
      </div>
      {phase === "recording" && (
        <p role="timer" aria-live="off" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-[#A13F36]">
          <span aria-hidden="true" className="h-2.5 w-2.5 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-[#C64D43]" />
          {t("voice.recorder.recording", { time: formatClock(elapsed), max: formatClock(maxMs) })}
        </p>
      )}
      {phase === "recorded" && <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">{t("voice.recorder.recorded", { time: formatClock(elapsed) })}</p>}
      <p className="sr-only" aria-live="polite">{announcement}</p>
      {problem && (
        <div role="alert" className="break-words rounded-xl border border-[#F2CDC7] bg-[#FFF8F6] px-3.5 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[#75413D]">
          <p>{t(problem.key)}</p>
          {problem.plan === "premium" && typeof problem.limit === "number" && <p className="mt-1">{t("voice.limit.premiumNote", { limit: problem.limit })}</p>}
          {problem.plan === "free" && <p className="mt-1">{t("voice.limit.upgrade")} <Link to="/learner/plan" className="font-extrabold underline underline-offset-2">{t("voice.limit.upgradeLink")}</Link></p>}
          {problem.offerTyping && onTypeInstead && (
            <button type="button" onClick={onTypeInstead} className={`${button} mt-2 border border-[#E9BDB6] bg-white text-[#8D342D]`}>
              <Keyboard aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />{t("voice.typeInstead")}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

  Create `parle-app/src/components/parle/SpeakingResultCard.tsx` (editor tool):

```tsx
import { t, type TranslationKey } from "@/lib/i18n";
import type { SpeakingResult } from "@/lib/voice-client";

const ROWS: Array<[keyof SpeakingResult["criteria"], TranslationKey]> = [
  ["task_completion", "voice.result.taskCompletion"],
  ["vocabulary", "voice.result.vocabulary"],
  ["grammar", "voice.result.grammar"],
  ["coherence_fluency", "voice.result.coherence"],
];

/** Spec 4.2: what we heard, four criteria, the practice estimate with NCLC, up to three corrections, one tip. */
export function SpeakingResultCard({ result }: { result: SpeakingResult }) {
  return (
    <section aria-label={t("voice.result.estimate")} className="min-w-0 space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#B9D9CB] bg-[#F0F8F4] p-4 sm:p-5">
      {result.mode === "typed" && <p className="rounded-xl bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold leading-5 text-[#205D45]">{t("voice.result.typed")}</p>}
      <div>
        <h4 className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-[0.13em] text-[#287252]">{t("voice.result.heard")}</h4>
        <p lang="fr" className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-sm leading-6 text-foreground [overflow-wrap:anywhere]">{result.transcript}</p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <h4 className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-[0.13em] text-[#287252]">{t("voice.result.criteria")}</h4>
        <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
          {ROWS.map(([key, label]) => (
            <div key={key} className="min-w-0 rounded-xl border border-[#B9D9CB]/70 bg-white p-3">
              <p className="break-words text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-muted-foreground">{t(label)}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 font-display text-xl font-extrabold text-primary">{result.criteria[key]}<span className="ml-1 text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">/ 4</span></p>
              {key === "coherence_fluency" && result.wordsPerMinute !== null && <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{t("voice.result.rate", { wpm: result.wordsPerMinute })}</p>}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-3">
        <p className="font-display text-lg font-extrabold text-primary">{t("voice.result.level", { level: result.level, nclc: result.nclc })}</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-bold text-[#8D342D]">{t("voice.result.estimate")}</p>
      </div>
      {result.corrections.length > 0 && (
        <div>
          <h4 className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-[0.13em] text-[#287252]">{t("voice.result.corrections")}</h4>
          <ul className="mt-2 space-y-2">
            {result.corrections.map((c, i) => (
              <li key={i} className="break-words rounded-xl bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm leading-6 [overflow-wrap:anywhere]">
                <p lang="fr">{t("voice.result.correction", { said: c.said, better: c.better })}</p>
                {c.reason && <p dir="auto" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{c.reason}</p>}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}
      <div>
        <h4 className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-[0.13em] text-[#287252]">{t("voice.result.tip")}</h4>
        <p dir="auto" className="mt-1 break-words text-sm leading-6 text-foreground">{result.tip}</p>
      </div>
      <p className="text-[11px] leading-5 text-muted-foreground">{t("voice.result.pronunciation")}</p>
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { LoaderCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { VoiceRecorder } from "@/components/parle/VoiceRecorder";
import { SpeakingResultCard } from "@/components/parle/SpeakingResultCard";
import { t, type TranslationKey } from "@/lib/i18n";
import type { LearnerTaskView } from "@/lib/learner-plan-remote";
import { ANSWER_MAX_MS } from "@/lib/voice-audio";
import { loadTaskSpeakingResult, scoreSpokenAnswer, scoreTypedAnswer, voiceErrorFrom, voiceErrorKey, type ScoreOutcome, type SpeakingResult } from "@/lib/voice-client";

/** Spec 4.1: the speaking task card shows the prompt (above), Record, a timer, Stop, Re-record, Send, then the result. */
export function SpeakingTaskPanel({ task, onScored, disabled }: { task: LearnerTaskView; onScored: () => void; disabled: boolean }) {
  const [result, setResult] = useState<SpeakingResult | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<TranslationKey | null>(null);
  const [typing, setTyping] = useState(false);
  const [typed, setTyped] = useState("");
  const open = task.status === "planned" || task.status === "rolled";

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    if (!open && !result) void loadTaskSpeakingResult(task.id).then((saved) => { if (active) setResult(saved); }).catch(() => undefined);
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [task.id, open, result]);

  async function score(run: () => Promise<ScoreOutcome>, typedAnswer: boolean) {
    setBusy(true);
    setProblem(null);
    try {
      const outcome = await run();
      if (outcome.status === "scored") { setResult(outcome.result); onScored(); }
      else setProblem(outcome.status === "not_french" ? "voice.notFrench" : typedAnswer ? "voice.task.typedTooShort" : "voice.tooQuiet");
    } catch (error) {
      setProblem(voiceErrorKey(voiceErrorFrom(error), "answer"));
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  if (result) return <div className="mt-4"><SpeakingResultCard result={result} /></div>;
  if (!open) return null;
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby={`speak-${task.id}`} className="mt-4 min-w-0 rounded-2xl border border-primary/10 bg-white p-4">
      <h4 id={`speak-${task.id}`} className="font-display text-sm font-extrabold text-primary">{t("voice.task.title")}</h4>
      <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">{t("voice.task.intro")}</p>
      <div className="mt-3">
        {busy ? (
          <p role="status" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-primary"><LoaderCircle aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />{t("voice.task.scoring")}</p>
        ) : typing ? (
          <form onSubmit={(e: FormEvent) => { e.preventDefault(); void score(() => scoreTypedAnswer(task.id, typed), true); }} className="space-y-2">
            <label htmlFor={`typed-${task.id}`} className="block text-xs font-bold text-foreground">{t("voice.task.typedLabel")}</label>
            <textarea id={`typed-${task.id}`} lang="fr" rows={5} maxLength={4000} value={typed} onChange={(e) => setTyped(e.target.value)} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-input bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm leading-6 outline-none focus:border-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/15" />
            <button type="submit" disabled={disabled || !typed.trim()} className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-full bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-white disabled:opacity-60">{t("voice.task.typedSubmit")}</button>
          </form>
        ) : (
          <VoiceRecorder purpose="answer" refId={task.id} maxMs={ANSWER_MAX_MS} disabled={disabled} onTranscribed={(r) => void score(() => scoreSpokenAnswer(r.ticketId, task.id), false)} onTypeInstead={() => setTyping(true)} />
        )}
      </div>
      {problem && <p role="alert" className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-[#F2CDC7] bg-[#FFF8F6] px-3.5 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[#75413D]">{t(problem)}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { frenchChunks } from "./voice-text";
import { speakChunk, VoiceRequestError } from "./voice-client";

export type PlayOutcome = "natural" | "browser" | "browser_limit" | "none" | "unavailable" | "stopped";

/**
 * Spec 5: the French sentences of a reply, spoken in order in the natural voice; Stop at any time; if the voice is
 * unavailable (limit reached or provider down) the browser's own voice reads, with a note.
 */
export class ReplyPlayer {
  private audio: HTMLAudioElement | null = null;
  private urls: string[] = [];
  private run = 0;

  stop(): void {
    this.run++;
    this.audio?.pause();
    this.audio = null;
    for (const url of this.urls) URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    this.urls = [];
    if (typeof window !== "undefined" && "speechSynthesis" in window) window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  }

  async play(text: string, replyId: string, rate = 1): Promise<PlayOutcome> {
    this.stop();
    const run = this.run;
    const chunks = frenchChunks(text);
    if (!chunks.length) return "none";
    let index = 0;
    if (replyId) {
      try {
        for (; index < chunks.length; index++) {
          const blob = await speakChunk(chunks[index], replyId);
          if (run !== this.run) return "stopped";
          await this.playBlob(blob, rate);
          if (run !== this.run) return "stopped";
        }
        return "natural";
      } catch (error) {
        if (run !== this.run) return "stopped";
        return this.browser(chunks.slice(index), rate, error instanceof VoiceRequestError && error.code === "LIMIT_REACHED" ? "browser_limit" : "browser");
      }
    }
    return this.browser(chunks, rate, "browser");
  }

  private playBlob(blob: Blob, rate: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      this.urls.push(url);
      const audio = new Audio(url);
      audio.playbackRate = rate;
      this.audio = audio;
      audio.onended = () => resolve();
      audio.onerror = () => reject(new VoiceRequestError("VOICE_BUSY"));
      void audio.play().catch(reject);
    });
  }

  private browser(chunks: string[], rate: number, outcome: "browser" | "browser_limit"): PlayOutcome {
    if (typeof window === "undefined" || !("speechSynthesis" in window) || typeof SpeechSynthesisUtterance === "undefined") return "unavailable";
    const voice = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices().find((v) => /^fr(?:[-_]|$)/i.test(v.lang));
    if (!voice) return "unavailable";
    for (const chunk of chunks) {
      const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(chunk);
      utterance.voice = voice;
      utterance.lang = voice.lang || "fr-FR";
      utterance.rate = rate === 1 ? 1 : 0.72;
      window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
    }
    return outcome;
  }
}
```

  Create `parle-app/src/components/parle/ListeningAudioButton.tsx` (editor tool):

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { LoaderCircle, Volume2 } from "lucide-react";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { listeningUrl } from "@/lib/voice-client";

/** Spec 5: listening items with a script get natural French audio, made once and reused; never counted. */
export function ListeningAudioButton({ kind, id }: { kind: "question_item" | "learner_task"; id: string }) {
  const [url, setUrl] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => { setUrl(""); setFailed(false); }, [kind, id]);
  if (url) return <audio controls autoPlay preload="auto" src={url} className="mt-2 w-full">{t("voice.listening.unavailable")}</audio>;
  return (
    <div className="mt-2">
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={busy}
        onClick={async () => {
          setBusy(true);
          setFailed(false);
          try { setUrl(await listeningUrl(kind, id)); } catch { setFailed(true); } finally { setBusy(false); }
        }}
        className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-primary/15 bg-white px-4 text-xs font-extrabold text-primary transition hover:bg-[#F7F8FB] focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent disabled:cursor-wait disabled:opacity-60"
      >
        {busy ? <LoaderCircle aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Volume2 aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />}
        {t(busy ? "voice.listening.loading" : "voice.listening.play")}
      </button>
      {failed && <p role="status" className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">{t("voice.listening.unavailable")}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

  Create `parle-app/src/components/parle/AdminListeningAudio.tsx` (editor tool):

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { LoaderCircle, Volume2 } from "lucide-react";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { listeningUrl, loadListeningMissing } from "@/lib/voice-client";

/** Spec 5: an admin "prepare audio" action fills the listening cache ahead of time (after the Lesson Bank import). */
export function AdminListeningAudio() {
  const [state, setState] = useState<"loading" | "ready" | "error">("loading");
  const [missing, setMissing] = useState<Array<{ item_kind: "question_item" | "learner_task"; item_id: string }>>([]);
  const [total, setTotal] = useState(0);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState<{ done: number; failed: number; count: number } | null>(null);
  const [running, setRunning] = useState(false);

  async function load() {
    setState("loading");
    try {
      const r = await loadListeningMissing();
      setMissing(r.missing);
      setTotal(r.total);
      setState("ready");
    } catch {
      setState("error");
    }
  }
  useEffect(() => { void load(); }, []);

  async function prepare() {
    setRunning(true);
    let done = 0, failed = 0;
    const count = missing.length;
    setProgress({ done, failed, count });
    for (const item of missing) {
      try { await listeningUrl(item.item_kind, item.item_id); done++; } catch { failed++; }
      setProgress({ done, failed, count });
    }
    setRunning(false);
    await load();
  }

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="admin-listening-title" className="min-w-0 rounded-[1.35rem] border border-[#E1E5EC] bg-white p-4 shadow-[0_14px_34px_-30px_rgba(23,43,91,0.45)] sm:p-5">
      <h3 id="admin-listening-title" className="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-base font-extrabold text-[#1E315C]"><Volume2 aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4" />{t("adminListening.title")}</h3>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-[#5B667C]">{t("adminListening.intro")}</p>
      {state === "loading" && <p role="status" className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-[#5B667C]"><LoaderCircle aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /></p>}
      {state === "error" && <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-sm text-[#8D342D]">{t("adminListening.loadFailed")}</p>}
      {state === "ready" && (
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-[#1E315C]">{missing.length ? t("adminListening.missing", { count: missing.length, total }) : t("adminListening.allReady")}</p>
          {missing.length > 0 && (
            <button type="button" onClick={() => void prepare()} disabled={running} className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#1E3A8A] px-4 text-sm font-bold text-white disabled:opacity-60">
              {running && <LoaderCircle aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />}{t("adminListening.prepare")}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      {progress && (
        <p role="status" className="mt-2 text-xs text-[#5B667C]">
          {t("adminListening.progress", { done: progress.done, count: progress.count })}
          {progress.failed > 0 && ` ${t("adminListening.failedCount", { count: progress.failed })}`}
        </p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { describe, it } from "node:test";
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, NUMERIC_SETTINGS, validateDraft } from "../pages/AdminControlPanel";
import { APP_SETTING_DEFAULTS, APP_SETTING_RULES } from "../../functions/parle-admin-settings.helper.ts";

const VOICE = ["free_voice_answers_per_day", "premium_voice_answers_per_day", "free_voice_messages_per_day", "premium_voice_messages_per_day", "free_voice_replies_per_day", "premium_voice_replies_per_day"] as const;

describe("Admin Settings: voice limits (spec 6)", () => {
  it("shows the six voice limits with the server's defaults and ranges", () => {
    for (const key of VOICE) {
      const field = NUMERIC_SETTINGS.find((f) => f.key === key);
      assert.ok(field, key);
      assert.deepEqual([field!.min, field!.max, DEFAULT_SETTINGS[key]], [APP_SETTING_RULES[key].min, APP_SETTING_RULES[key].max, APP_SETTING_DEFAULTS[key]]);
    }
  });
  it("refuses a Premium limit below the Free one", () => {
    assert.equal(validateDraft({ ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, free_voice_replies_per_day: 30, premium_voice_replies_per_day: 10 }), "adminControl.error.premiumBelowFree");
    assert.equal(validateDraft({ ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS }), null);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The French sample.** Create `parle-backend/scripts/prod/voice-sample.ts` (editor tool):

```ts
// @ts-nocheck -- run with Node (npx tsx): one NVIDIA call makes a French sample for the browser's fake microphone
import { writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { nvidiaProvider } from "../../../parle-app/api/_voice/nvidia.ts";
import { pcmWav } from "../../../parle-app/api/_voice/wav.ts";
import { resample } from "../../../parle-app/src/lib/voice-audio.ts";
import { readSecret } from "../lib/secrets";

/** Writes parle-voice-sample-22k.wav (fake microphone) and parle-voice-sample-16k.wav (relay checks) to the temp folder. */
const TEXT = "Le week-end dernier, je suis allé au marché avec ma sœur. Nous avons acheté des légumes frais et du pain. Ensuite, nous avons pris un café sur la terrasse parce qu'il faisait très beau. J'aime beaucoup ces moments en famille.";
const speech = await nvidiaProvider(readSecret("NVIDIA_API_KEY")).synthesize(TEXT);
const padded = new Int16Array(speech.length + 2 * 22050);
padded.set(speech, 22050); // one second of silence before and after
writeFileSync(`${tmpdir()}/parle-voice-sample-22k.wav`, pcmWav(padded, 22050));
const low = resample(Float32Array.from(padded, (v) => v / 32768), 22050, 16000);
writeFileSync(`${tmpdir()}/parle-voice-sample-16k.wav`, pcmWav(Int16Array.from(low, (v) => Math.round(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, v)) * 32767)), 16000));
console.log(`sample: ${(speech.length / 22050).toFixed(1)} s of speech written to the temp folder`);
process.exit(0);
```

  Run: `cd D:/AgentDevWork/repos/Parle/07_Engineering/parle-backend && npx tsx scripts/prod/voice-sample.ts`
  Expected: `sample: 1x.x s of speech written to the temp folder` (about 15 s).

- [ ] **Step 3: API checks script.** Create `parle-backend/scripts/prod/plan9-checks.ts` (editor tool):

```ts
import { existsSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { createClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { PROD_REF, runSql, serviceKey } from "./management";
import { PROD_URL, anonKey, chat, fn, newLearner, removeLearner, type Learner } from "./check-learners";
import { readSecret, SECRETS_DIR } from "../lib/secrets";
import { frenchChunks } from "../../../parle-app/src/lib/voice-text";

/**
 * Plan 9 checks on production through the private preview's relay, with an AgentMail learner (obsidianteamhq+p9...).
 * Prints outcomes only: never passwords, tokens, signed links or keys. State in the secrets folder.
 * usage: bun scripts/prod/plan9-checks.ts <learner|reply|listening|limit|status|cleanup>
 */
const APP = "https://parle-preview-shayans-projects-d00acc44.vercel.app";
const STATE = `${SECRETS_DIR}/plan9-check-state.json`;
type State = { learner?: Learner & { id: string } };
const load = (): State => (existsSync(STATE) ? JSON.parse(readFileSync(STATE, "utf8")) : {});
const save = (s: State) => writeFileSync(STATE, JSON.stringify(s, null, 2));
const service = async () => createClient(PROD_URL, await serviceKey(PROD_REF), { auth: { persistSession: false } });
const today = () => new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { timeZone: "America/Toronto", year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit" }).format(new Date());

async function tokenFor(l: Learner): Promise<string> {
  const c = createClient(PROD_URL, await anonKey(), { auth: { persistSession: false } });
  const { data, error } = await c.auth.signInWithPassword({ email: l.email, password: l.password });
  if (error || !data.session) throw new Error(`sign-in failed: ${error?.message ?? "no session"}`);
  return data.session.access_token;
}
async function relay(token: string, query: string, body: BodyInit, json: boolean) {
  const r = await fetch(`${APP}/api/voice?${query}`, { method: "POST", headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "x-vercel-protection-bypass": readSecret("VERCEL_BYPASS"), "Content-Type": json ? "application/json" : "audio/wav" }, body });
  const type = r.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
  return { status: r.status, type, body: type.includes("json") ? ((await r.json()) as Record<string, any>) : null };
}
/** Turns one of the check learner's own tasks into an open speaking task dated today (check account only). */
async function openSpeakingTaskToday(userId: string, except: string[] = []): Promise<string> {
  const db = await service();
  const { data } = await db.from("learner_task").select("id, type, status").eq("owner_id", userId).in("status", ["planned", "rolled"]).order("created_at").limit(50);
  const rows = (data ?? []).filter((x) => !except.includes(String(x.id)));
  const task = rows.find((x) => x.type === "speaking_check" || x.type === "speaking_practice") ?? rows.find((x) => x.type !== "weekly_checkin" && x.type !== "tutor_lesson");
  if (!task) throw new Error("no open task to use");
  const { error } = await db.from("learner_task").update({ type: task.type.startsWith("speaking") ? task.type : "speaking_practice", skill: "speaking", status: "planned", date_local: today() }).eq("id", task.id);
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
  return String(task.id);
}

const steps: Record<string, () => Promise<void>> = {
  learner: async () => {
    const l = await newLearner("voice", "A2", "English", false, "p9");
    const c = createClient(PROD_URL, await anonKey(), { auth: { persistSession: false } });
    const id = (await c.auth.getUser(l.token)).data.user?.id ?? "";
    const plan = await fn("parle-plan-service", l.token, { action: "get_or_create_plan", options: {} });
    console.log(`learner: ready, plan ${plan.status}`);
    const first = await openSpeakingTaskToday(id); // for the recorded answer
    const second = await openSpeakingTaskToday(id, [first]); // for the microphone-refused check
    console.log(`learner: two speaking tasks for today ${Boolean(first && second && first !== second)}`);
    save({ learner: { ...l, id } });
  },
  reply: async () => {
    const l = load().learner!;
    const token = await tokenFor(l);
    await chat({ ...l, token }, "chat", ["Bonjour ! Je voudrais pratiquer le passé composé avec un exemple sur le travail."]);
    const db = await service();
    const { data } = await db.from("ai_message").select("id, text").eq("owner_id", l.id).eq("sender", "tutor_ai").order("created_at", { ascending: false }).limit(1);
    const reply = data?.[0];
    const chunk = reply ? frenchChunks(String(reply.text))[0] : undefined;
    if (!reply || !chunk) { console.log("reply: the tutor's reply had no French sentence to read (recorded, not a failure)"); return; }
    for (let i = 1; i <= 2; i++) {
      const r = await relay(token, "action=speak", JSON.stringify({ text: chunk, reply_id: reply.id }), true);
      console.log(`reply read aloud ${i}: ${r.status} ${r.type}`);
    }
    const s = await fn("parle-voice-service", token, { action: "voice_status" });
    console.log(`reply: replies used ${s.body.replies?.used} of ${s.body.replies?.limit}`);
  },
  listening: async () => {
    const token = await tokenFor(load().learner!);
    const rows = (await runSql(PROD_REF, "select id from public.question_item where skill = 'listening' and status = 'approved' and coalesce(audio_url, '') = '' and coalesce(transcript, '') <> '' order by demo_code limit 1")) as Array<{ id: string }>;
    if (!rows[0]) { console.log("listening: no listening item with a script"); return; }
    for (let i = 1; i <= 2; i++) {
      const r = await relay(token, "action=listen", JSON.stringify({ item_kind: "question_item", item_id: rows[0].id }), true);
      const audio = r.body?.url ? await fetch(r.body.url) : null;
      console.log(`listening ${i}: ${r.status} generated ${r.body?.generated}; file ${audio?.status} ${audio?.headers.get("content-type")}`);
    }
  },
  limit: async () => {
    const l = load().learner!;
    const token = await tokenFor(l);
    const taskId = await openSpeakingTaskToday(l.id);
    const wav = readFileSync(`${tmpdir()}/parle-voice-sample-16k.wav`);
    for (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
      const r = await relay(token, `action=transcribe&purpose=answer&ref=${taskId}&part=1`, wav, false);
      console.log(`limit answer ${i}: ${r.status} ${r.body?.error_code ?? ""} plan ${r.body?.plan ?? ""} French heard ${/marché|famille|café/i.test(String(r.body?.transcript ?? ""))}`);
    }
  },
  status: async () => {
    const s = await fn("parle-voice-service", await tokenFor(load().learner!), { action: "voice_status" });
    console.log(`status: ${s.status} plan ${s.body.plan}; answers ${s.body.answers?.used}/${s.body.answers?.limit}; messages ${s.body.messages?.used}/${s.body.messages?.limit}; replies ${s.body.replies?.used}/${s.body.replies?.limit}; zone ${s.body.time_zone}`);
  },
  cleanup: async () => {
    const l = load().learner!;
    await removeLearner({ ...l, token: await tokenFor(l) });
    const left = (await runSql(PROD_REF, `select (select count(*) from public.voice_use where user_id = '${l.id}')::int as uses, (select count(*) from public.speaking_result where user_id = '${l.id}')::int as results`)) as Array<Record<string, number>>;
    console.log(`cleanup: voice uses left ${left[0]?.uses}, results left ${left[0]?.results}`);
  },
};

const step = process.argv[2] ?? "";
if (!steps[step]) throw new Error(`usage: bun scripts/prod/plan9-checks.ts <${Object.keys(steps).join("|")}>`);
await steps[step]();
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Browser checks script.** Create `parle-backend/scripts/prod/plan9-preview-checks.ts` (editor tool):

```ts
// @ts-nocheck -- run with Node (npx tsx); Playwright comes from the gstack install, not a project dependency
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { chromium } from "file:///C:/Users/AgentDev/.claude/skills/gstack/node_modules/playwright/index.mjs";
import { readSecret, SECRETS_DIR } from "../lib/secrets";

/**
 * Plan 9 browser checks on the private preview: Chromium's fake microphone plays the NVIDIA French sample.
 * Outcomes only, never passwords or the bypass. usage: npx tsx scripts/prod/plan9-preview-checks.ts <speaking-task|mic-refused|tutor>
 */
const APP = "https://parle-preview-shayans-projects-d00acc44.vercel.app";
const FAKE_MIC = ["--use-fake-ui-for-media-stream", "--use-fake-device-for-media-stream", `--use-file-for-fake-audio-capture=${tmpdir()}/parle-voice-sample-22k.wav%noloop`];
const learner = () => JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${SECRETS_DIR}/plan9-check-state.json`, "utf8")).learner;
const bodyText = async (page) => (await page.locator("body").innerText()).replace(/\s+/g, " ");
const wide = (page) => page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth);

async function open(args, width) {
  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true, args });
  const context = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width, height: 900 } });
  const page = await context.newPage();
  const errors = [], voice = [];
  page.on("console", (m) => { if (m.type() === "error") errors.push(m.text().slice(0, 160)); });
  page.on("response", (r) => { if (r.url().includes("/api/voice")) voice.push(`${r.status()} ${r.headers()["content-type"] ?? ""}`); });
  await page.goto(`${APP}/?x-vercel-protection-bypass=${readSecret("VERCEL_BYPASS")}&x-vercel-set-bypass-cookie=true`);
  const l = learner();
  await page.goto(`${APP}/login`);
  await page.fill("input[type=email]", l.email);
  await page.fill("input[type=password]", l.password);
  await page.press("input[type=password]", "Enter");
  await page.waitForTimeout(8000);
  return { browser, page, errors, voice };
}
async function record(page, seconds, sendLabel) {
  const button = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Record your answer" }).first();
  await button.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  await button.click();
  await page.waitForTimeout(seconds * 1000);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" }).first().click();
  await page.waitForTimeout(3000);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: sendLabel }).first().click();
}

const checks = {
  "speaking-task": async () => {
    const s = await open(FAKE_MIC, 375);
    try {
      await s.page.goto(`${APP}/learner/today`);
      await s.page.waitForTimeout(8000);
      await record(s.page, 18, "Send");
      await s.page.getByText("What we heard").first().waitFor({ timeout: 60000 });
      const text = await bodyText(s.page);
      console.log(`speaking-task 375px: result shown true; French heard ${/marché|famille|café/i.test(text)}; estimate label ${text.includes("Practice estimate, not an official result")}; pronunciation line ${text.includes("Pronunciation isn't scored yet.")}; scrollWidth ${await wide(s.page)}; relay answers ${s.voice.join(", ")}; console errors ${s.errors.length}`);
    } finally { await s.browser.close(); }
  },
  "mic-refused": async () => {
    const s = await open(["--deny-permission-prompts"], 375);
    try {
      await s.page.goto(`${APP}/learner/today`);
      await s.page.waitForTimeout(8000);
      const button = s.page.getByRole("button", { name: "Record your answer" }).first();
      await button.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
      await button.click();
      await s.page.waitForTimeout(3000);
      console.log(`mic-refused: blocked message ${(await bodyText(s.page)).includes("The microphone is blocked")}`);
      await s.page.getByRole("button", { name: "Type my answer instead" }).first().click();
      await s.page.locator("textarea[lang=fr]").first().fill("Le week-end dernier, je suis allé au marché avec ma sœur et nous avons acheté du pain frais.");
      await s.page.getByRole("button", { name: "Score my typed answer" }).first().click();
      await s.page.getByText("Typed answer: scored as writing practice").first().waitFor({ timeout: 60000 });
      console.log(`mic-refused: typed answer scored and marked as writing practice true; scrollWidth ${await wide(s.page)}; console errors ${s.errors.length}`);
    } finally { await s.browser.close(); }
  },
  tutor: async () => {
    const s = await open(FAKE_MIC, 375);
    try {
      await s.page.goto(`${APP}/learner/ai-tutor?mode=speaking`);
      await s.page.waitForTimeout(20000);
      await s.page.getByRole("button", { name: "Listen" }).first().click();
      await s.page.waitForTimeout(10000);
      console.log(`tutor: Listen used the natural voice ${s.voice.some((v) => v.startsWith("200 audio/mpeg"))}`);
      await record(s.page, 8, "Use this recording");
      await s.page.waitForTimeout(15000);
      const typed = await s.page.locator("textarea").first().inputValue();
      console.log(`tutor: voice message written into the box ${typed.trim().length > 0}; French heard ${/marché|famille|café/i.test(typed)}; scrollWidth ${await wide(s.page)}; console errors ${s.errors.length}`);
    } finally { await s.browser.close(); }
  },
};

const name = process.argv[2] ?? "";
if (!checks[name]) throw new Error(`usage: npx tsx scripts/prod/plan9-preview-checks.ts <${Object.keys(checks).join("|")}>`);
await checks[name]();
```

## Self-review notes (writer)

- **Spec coverage.**
  - 1 purpose: scored speaking (T5, T6, T9), tutor voice both ways (T4, T10), listening audio (T7, T10), limits for Free
    and Premium (T1, T3, T11).
  - 2 decisions: NVIDIA Whisper and Magpie Louise, France French (T4 `nvidia.ts`); the Node relay on Vercel because gRPC
    does not run in Supabase functions (T4); the four surfaces (T9, T10); limits 2/15, 5/60, 20/200, placement and
    cached listening never counted (T1 SQL, T3, T7); recordings transcribed then discarded, transcripts stored (T1,
    T3 `voicePart`, T5 clears the ticket's transcript after copying it to the result).
  - 3 relay: `/api/voice` with speak and transcribe (T4) and listen (T7); token checked through `getCaller()`, paused
    accounts refused (T3 test, T4 backend test; ruling 3); the NVIDIA key only in Vercel (T14); a unit taken before
    each NVIDIA call through the backend with a shared secret, counters in the database (T1, T3, T4); 3 minutes / about
    5 MB (parts, ruling 2), about 1,000 characters (T2 chunks, T4 1,100 cap), 30 s timeout and "Voice is busy" (T4);
    WebM/Opus and MP4/AAC converted to 16 kHz mono (browser, ruling 1, T8); the fake provider in tests (T4).
  - 4.1 where: speaking tasks with prompt, Record, timer, Stop, Re-record, Send (T8 `VoiceRecorder`, T9); placement on
    its speaking prompts, replacing "speaking not scored" (T6, T9). 4.2 the result: transcript, four criteria with
    speaking rate, level with NCLC and "practice estimate", up to three corrections in the explanation language, one
    tip, "Pronunciation isn't scored yet." (T5 prompt and shape, T8 card). 4.3 rules: under 10 words or silent, not
    French, re-record free and each sent answer counted once, task done and estimate feeding the plan, placement sets
    the starting level, `ai_model_standard` with a strict shape retried once, stored fields with no audio (T5, T6).
  - 5 tutor and listening: Listen on every reply, French sentences only, Stop (T2, T10); "Read replies aloud" off by
    default and remembered (T3, T10); browser-voice fallback with a note (T10); microphone up to 1 minute with the
    transcript in the box before sending and counted as a voice message (T3, T10, ruling 13); listening audio made
    once, cached in Storage keyed by item and script, real recordings kept, admin "prepare audio" (T7, T10);
    accessibility: labelled buttons, a visible timer, permission asked on Record with the reason, typing offered when
    refused and scored as writing practice (T8, T9).
  - 6 limits and settings: the six keys, per learner day in their time zone, checked on the server, the limit message
    with the Premium limit or the upgrade hint, the uncounted cases (T1, T3, T8, T11).
  - 7 privacy: not stored, not logged; transcripts deleted with the account (T5 and T7 tests); the privacy paragraph
    shown to the founder before publishing, lawyer review noted (T12, T16).
  - 8 build, testing, checks: one migration (T1); tests for too short, not French, limit reached, paused account
    refused by the relay, bad token, Safari's format (T4 handler test and T8 `pickRecordingType`), the scoring shape,
    a cached item served twice with one generation (T3-T8); the real-system checks (T15); the final review before the
    deploy (T13); the founder's Vercel step (T14).
  - 9 not in Plan 9: no pronunciation scoring, no live voice, no Canadian voices, no replay of one's own recordings
    (the recorder has no playback), no tutor or admin access to recordings (none are stored).
  - 10 risks: NVIDIA terms asked (T15 Step 1, T16); mishearing (transcript always shown, editable in the tutor); no
    pronunciation (every result says so); Vercel limits (60 s parts, `maxDuration` 60, ruling 2); two key locations
    (T16 security note).
- **Placeholders:** none. Values filled at run time: the deployment URL printed by Vercel (T14 Step 6), dates in the
  records, the founder's wording changes if any (T12 Step 5).
- **Type consistency:** `voice_take` answers `{ ok, ticket_id, used, again }` (T1) read by `voiceTake` (T3); relay
  actions `voice_take` / `voice_part` / `voice_release` / `listening_claim` / `listening_store` (T3, T7) called by
  `backendFor` (T4) with the field names `kind, ref, part, ticket_id, text, transcript, duration_ms, item_kind,
  item_id, cache_key, audio_base64`; `voice_part` answers `{ transcript, words, duration_ms, total_ms }` used by the
  relay (T4) and summed by `transcribe()` (T8); `SpeakingResult` server shape (T5: `words_per_minute`, `duration_ms`,
  `nclc`) parsed by `safeSpeakingResult` (T8: `wordsPerMinute`, `durationMs`); `SpeakingAssessment` (T6) used by
  `placementScoreSpeaking`, `scoreSpeaking` and `resultFromAssessment` (T6, T8, T9); `MAX_PARTS = 4` (T3) matches the
  relay's part cap (T4) and the migration's `parts between 0 and 4` (T1); `ANSWER_MAX_MS`, `MESSAGE_MAX_MS` (T8) used
  by T9 and T10; settings keys identical in the helper (T3), the backend reader (T3), the page (T11) and the tests.
- **Review Focus:** each line has its named test in the owning task: 1 in T2, T8 and T5; 2 in T5; 3 in T3; 4 in T3 and
  T8; 5 in T3.
